&\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2+\epsilon}\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\|(\mathcal{N}+1)^{(m-1)/2}\Theta(\mathcal{L}_{n}/N^{\alpha})\zeta_{s}\|\|(\mathcal{N}+1)^{m/2}\Theta(\mathcal{L}_{n}/N^{\alpha})\zeta_{s}\|\end{split}

(101)

for any
\epsilon>0
, if
n
is large enough. Here we proceeded similarly as in (
99
), decomposing
\check{A}
into a sum of
\check{A}_{u,v}
, for
u,v\in\Lambda_{B}
. With (
100
), we conclude that

\begin{split}\langle\exp(A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ )&\zeta,\mathcal{N}^{m}\exp(A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ )\zeta\rangle\\
&\leq\langle\zeta,\mathcal{N}^{m}\zeta\rangle+CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2+\epsilon}N^{(3\kappa/2-\beta/4+\epsilon)(m-1/2)}\\
&\leq\langle\zeta,\mathcal{N}^{m}\zeta\rangle+CN^{m(1-3\beta/4+2\epsilon)}\leq\langle\zeta,\mathcal{N}^{m}\zeta\rangle+CN^{m(1-\beta/2)}\end{split}

if
n
is large enough (depending on
\alpha>0
).

Finally, we prove (
97
). Since

\int dw\,\big(\mathcal{N}_{\chi_{\ell_{B}}}(w)+1\big)^{n}a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}=a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}\int dw\,\big(\mathcal{N}_{\chi_{\ell_{B}}}(w)+\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x)+\chi_{\ell_{B}}(y)+\chi_{\ell_{B}}(z)+1\big)^{n}

we find

\begin{split}\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega,A^{*}(1-&\Theta)Ae^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega\rangle\\
=\;&\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega,\mathbbm{1}_{[1,\infty)}(\mathcal{L}_{n}/N^{\alpha})A^{*}(1-\Theta)Ae^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega\rangle.\end{split}

With
\|A^{*}(1-\Theta)A(\mathcal{N}+1)^{-3}\|\leq CN^{\tau}
, for some
\tau>0
, we conclude

\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega,A^{*}(1-\Theta)Ae^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega\rangle\leq CN^{\tau}\|\mathbbm{1}_{[1,\infty)}(\mathcal{L}_{n}/N^{\alpha})e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega\|\|\mathcal{N}^{3}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega\|.

From (
95
) (the fact that here we have
\mathbbm{1}_{[1,\infty)}
rather than
1-\Theta
does not change the argument) and (
96
) we obtain

\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega,A^{*}(1-\Theta)Ae^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega\rangle\leq CN^{-\nu}

if
\alpha>0
and then
n\in\mathbb{N}
are large enough.
∎

2.4
The number of particles in the trial state

With the coefficients
\mu_{p}
from (
39
), the coefficients
A_{p,q}
from (
62
) and the local particle number cutoff
\Theta=\Theta(\mathcal{L}_{n}/N^{\alpha})
introduced in Section
2.3
, we can define the quadratic operator
B
and the cubic operator
A
as in (
21
) and (
22
) and the trial state

\psi_{N}=W_{N_{0}}e^{B-B^{*}}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega

(102)

as in (
18
), with
W_{N_{0}}
the Weyl operator (
19
) generating the Bose-Einstein condensate. Here, we choose the condensate number of particles
N_{0}
so that

N=N_{0}+\sum_{p\in\Lambda_{+}^{*}}\sigma_{p}^{2}

with
\sigma_{p}=\sinh\mu_{p}
as defined in (
40
) (the norm
\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}
is the number of excitations generated by the Bogoliubov transformation). Since, from Lemma
5
,
\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}\ll N
for
\kappa<2/3
, it is always possible to find such
N_{0}
.

In the next lemma, we check that, with these definitions, our trial state has the correct number of particles. In the next section, we compute its energy.

Lemma 12
.

Consider the trial state (
102
). Then, we have

\langle\Psi_{N},\mathcal{N}\Psi_{N}\rangle\geq N,\quad\langle\Psi_{N},\mathcal{N}^{2}\Psi_{N}\rangle\leq CN^{2}

(103)

if
\alpha>0
and then
n\in\mathbb{N}
are large enough.

Proof.

With (
20
), we have

\begin{split}\langle\Psi_{N},\mathcal{N}\Psi_{N}\rangle&=\langle e^{B^{*}-B}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,(\mathcal{N}+N_{0}+\sqrt{N_{0}}(a_{0}^{*}+a_{0}))e^{B^{*}-B}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&=\langle e^{B^{*}-B}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,(\mathcal{N}+N_{0})e^{B^{*}-B}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\end{split}

because the expectation of
a_{0},a_{0}^{*}
is zero in the state
e^{B^{*}-B}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
. From (
40
), we find

e^{B-B^{*}}\mathcal{N}e^{B^{*}-B}=\sum_{p\in\Lambda_{+}^{*}}(\gamma_{p}^{2}+\sigma_{p}^{2})a_{p}^{*}a_{p}+\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\gamma_{p}\sigma_{p}(a_{p}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}+a_{p}a_{-p})+\sum_{p\in\Lambda_{+}^{*}}\sigma_{p}^{2}.

(104)

Since the expectation of
a_{p}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}
vanishes in the state
e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
, we immediately conclude that

\langle\Psi_{N},\mathcal{N}\Psi_{N}\rangle\geq N_{0}+\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\sigma_{p}^{2}=N.

On the other hand, recalling from Lemma
5
that
\|\sigma\|_{\infty},\|\gamma\|_{\infty}\leq CN^{\beta/8}
, (
104
) implies that

e^{B-B^{*}}\mathcal{N}e^{B^{*}-B}\leq CN^{\beta/4}\mathcal{N}+CN\,.

Similarly,

e^{B-B^{*}}\mathcal{N}^{2}e^{B^{*}-B}\leq CN^{\beta/2}\mathcal{N}^{2}+CN^{2}.

Thus

\begin{split}\langle\Psi_{N},\mathcal{N}^{2}\Psi_{N}\rangle&\leq C\langle e^{B^{*}-B}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,(\mathcal{N}^{2}+N^{2}_{0})e^{B^{*}-B}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&\leq C\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,(N^{\beta/2}\mathcal{N}^{2}+N^{2})e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&\leq CN^{\beta/2}N^{2-\beta}+CN^{2}\leq CN^{2}\end{split}

where in the last step we used the rough bound (
96
).
∎

3
Energy of the trial state

In this section, we are going to estimate the energy
\langle\psi_{N},\mathcal{H}_{N}\psi_{N}\rangle
of the trial state
\psi_{N}
defined in (
102
). Together with Lemma
12
, this will complete the proof of Theorem
2
. Throughout the section, we will use the notation
\xi=e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega
for the action of the cubic transformation on the vacuum, so that
\psi_{N}=W_{N_{0}}e^{B^{*}-B}\xi
.

3.1
Action of Weyl and Bogoliubov transformations on
\mathcal{H}_{N}

In the next lemma, we compute the action of
W_{N_{0}}
and of
e^{B^{*}-B}
on the Hamiltonian

\mathcal{H}_{N}=\mathcal{K}+\mathcal{V}_{N}=\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}a_{p}^{*}a_{p}+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})a_{p+r}^{*}a_{q-r}^{*}a_{q}a_{p}.

(105)

This will allow us to express the energy
\langle\psi_{N},\mathcal{H}_{N}\psi_{N}\rangle
in terms of certain expectations in the state
\xi
, which will be estimated in the next subsections.

Lemma 13
.

We have

\begin{split}\langle\Psi_{N},&\mathcal{H}_{N}\Psi_{N}\rangle\\
=\;&4\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{1+\kappa}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\Big(\sqrt{\lvert p\rvert^{4}+2p^{2}N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{p}{N^{1-\kappa}})}-p^{2}-N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{p}{N^{1-\kappa}})+\frac{N^{2\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{p}{N^{1-\kappa}})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}\Big)\\
&-\frac{N^{\kappa}\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{2}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}\sigma_{p}^{2}\\
&+\langle\xi,(\mathcal{K}+\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*}+\mathcal{C}_{N}+\mathcal{V}_{N})\xi\rangle+\langle\xi,(\mathcal{Q}+\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}^{*}+\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N})\xi\rangle+O(N^{4\kappa-1}+N^{2\kappa})\end{split}

(106)

where

\begin{split}\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*}&=\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{1/2}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p}a_{p+r}^{*}a_{-r}^{*}a_{-p}^{*},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}^{*}&=\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{1/2}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})((\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{r}-1)\sigma_{p}+\sigma_{p+r}\sigma_{r}\gamma_{p})a_{p+r}^{*}a_{-r}^{*}a_{-p}^{*},\\
\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}&=\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{q-r}\gamma_{q}\gamma_{p}-1+\sigma_{p+r}\sigma_{q-r}\sigma_{q}\sigma_{p}+2\gamma_{p+r}\sigma_{q-r}\sigma_{q}\gamma_{p})\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times a_{p+r}^{*}a_{q-r}^{*}a_{q}a_{p}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{N}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\gamma_{p+r}\sigma_{q-r}\gamma_{q}\sigma_{p}a_{p+r}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}a_{-q+r}a_{q}\end{split}

(107)

and
\mathcal{Q}
is a quadratic operator satisfying

\pm\mathcal{Q}\leq CN^{\kappa}(\mathcal{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{N}})

(108)

with
\tilde{\mathcal{N}}=\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\sigma_{p}^{2}a_{p}^{*}a_{p}
.

Remark:
in the next subsection, we will show that the expectations of the operators
\mathcal{Q},\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N},\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}
in the state
\xi=e^{A^{*}\Theta-\Theta A}\Omega
are all negligible (they only contribute to order
N^{4\kappa-1}
or lower).

Proof.

We proceed as in
[
4
, Section 3]
. With (
20
), we find

\langle\Psi_{N},\mathcal{H}_{N}\Psi_{N}\rangle=\sum_{n=0}^{4}\langle e^{B^{*}-B}\xi,\mathcal{W}_{n}e^{B^{*}-B}\xi\rangle

(109)

where

\begin{split}&\mathcal{W}_{0}=\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{2}}{2N}\hat{V}(0)\\
&\mathcal{W}_{1}=\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{3/2}}{N}\hat{V}(0)a_{0}^{*}+\text{h.c.}\ \\
&\mathcal{W}_{2}=\mathcal{K}+\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{2N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\left(a_{p}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}+a_{p}a_{-p}+2a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\right)+\frac{N^{\kappa}\hat{V}(0)N_{0}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\\
&\mathcal{W}_{3}=\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{1/2}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})a_{p+r}^{*}a_{-r}^{*}a_{p}+\text{h.c.}\ \\
&\mathcal{W}_{4}=\mathcal{V}_{N}.\end{split}

(110)

Setting
\mathcal{G}_{n}=e^{-B^{*}+B}\mathcal{W}_{n}e^{B^{*}-B}
for
n=0,\dots,4
, we find

\langle\Psi_{N},\mathcal{H}_{N}\Psi_{N}\rangle=\sum_{n=0}^{4}\langle\xi,\mathcal{G}_{n}\xi\rangle.

(111)

Clearly, we have
\langle\xi,\mathcal{G}_{0}\xi\rangle=N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{2}\hat{V}(0)/2N
and
\langle\xi,\mathcal{G}_{1}\xi\rangle=0
. Since
\langle\xi,a_{p}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}\xi\rangle=0
(because
A^{*}
only creates triples), we obtain

\begin{split}\langle\xi,\mathcal{G}_{2}\xi\rangle=\;&\langle\xi,\mathcal{K}\xi\rangle+\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}2p^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}\langle\xi,a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\xi\rangle+\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{p}+\sigma_{p})^{2}\langle\xi,a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\xi\rangle\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt+\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{p}\sigma_{p}+\sigma_{p}^{2})\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}\hat{V}(0)N_{0}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}(\gamma_{p}^{2}+\sigma_{p}^{2})\langle\xi,a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\xi\rangle+\frac{N^{\kappa}\hat{V}(0)N_{0}}{N}\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}\,.\end{split}

(112)

Taking into account that the expectation of operators having the form
a^{*}a^{*}a
and
a^{*}aa
in the state
\xi
is zero, we find

\langle\xi,\mathcal{G}_{3}\xi\rangle=\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{1/2}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{r}\sigma_{p}+\sigma_{p+r}\sigma_{r}\gamma_{p})\langle\xi,(a_{p+r}^{*}a_{-r}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}+\text{h.c.}\ )\xi\rangle.

(113)

Finally, since operators of the form
a^{*}a^{*}a^{*}a^{*}
,
a^{*}a^{*}a^{*}a
,
a^{*}aaa
,
aaaa
have vanishing expectation in the state
\xi
, we obtain

\begin{split}\langle\xi,\mathcal{G}_{4}\xi\rangle=\;&\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\big(\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{q-r}\gamma_{q}\gamma_{p}+\sigma_{p+r}\sigma_{q-r}\sigma_{q}\sigma_{p}+2\gamma_{p+r}\sigma_{q-r}\sigma_{q}\gamma_{p}\big)\\
&\hskip 256.0748pt\times\langle\xi,a_{p+r}^{*}a_{q-r}^{*}a_{q}a_{p}\xi\rangle\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{N}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\gamma_{p+r}\sigma_{q-r}\gamma_{q}\sigma_{p}\langle\xi,a_{p+r}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}a_{-q+r}a_{q}\xi\rangle\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{N}\hat{V}(0)\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{2}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}(\gamma_{p}^{2}+\sigma_{p}^{2})\langle\xi,a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\xi\rangle\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p+r}^{2}(\gamma_{p}^{2}+\sigma_{p}^{2})\langle\xi,a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\xi\rangle\\
&+\frac{2N^{\kappa}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p+r}\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{p}\sigma_{p}\langle\xi,a_{p}^{*}a_{p}\xi\rangle\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(\sigma_{p+r}\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{p}\sigma_{p}+\sigma_{p+r}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2})+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\hat{V}(0)\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{4}.\end{split}

(114)

Going through the r.h.s. of (
112
) and (
114
), we observe that all quadratic terms, with the exception of the kinetic energy operator
\mathcal{K}
, can be bounded as in (
108
) (here, we use the bounds from Lemma
5
, which imply, in particular, that
p^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}\leq CN^{\kappa}
, for all
p\in\Lambda^{*}
). The cubic terms in (
113
) correspond exactly to the sum of the two cubic terms defined in (
107
). Moreover, the quartic terms on the r.h.s. of (
114
) are clearly the sum of
\mathcal{V}_{N}
and
\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}
, with
\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}
as defined in (
107
). To complete the proof of Lemma
13
, we only have to check that the constant terms arising from
\mathcal{G}_{0},\mathcal{G}_{2},\mathcal{G}_{4}
match the r.h.s. of (
106
). To this end we notice, first of all, that the contribution
\langle\xi,\mathcal{G}_{0}\xi\rangle
can be combined with the last term on the r.h.s. of (
112
) and the last term on the r.h.s. of (
114
) to yield

\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}^{2}}{2N}\hat{V}(0)+\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{N}\hat{V}(0)\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\hat{V}(0)\|\sigma\|_{2}^{4}=\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\hat{V}(0)\big(N_{0}+\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{2}=\frac{N^{1+\kappa}}{2}\hat{V}(0).

Next, we observe that the term

\Big|\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p+r}^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}\Big|\leq CN^{\kappa-1}\|\hat{V}\|_{\infty}\|\sigma\|_{2}^{4}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}

appearing on the r.h.s. of (
114
) is negligible. The remaining constant term on the r.h.s. of (
114
) can be rewritten as

\begin{split}\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r}\hat{V}&(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p+r}\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{p}\sigma_{p}\\
=\;&\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{\infty,p+r}\gamma_{\infty,p+r}\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\end{split}

because the contribution associated with low momenta
|p|,|p+r|\leq N^{\kappa/2-\beta/4}
is negligible. Using the bound for
\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p}
in Lemma
3
, we obtain

\begin{split}\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p+r}&\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{p}\sigma_{p}\\
=\;&\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}\eta_{\infty,p}\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{N}\sum_{p,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}\big(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p}\big)+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

As for the remaining constant terms in (
112
), we write

\begin{split}&\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{N}\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\gamma_{p}\sigma_{p}\\
&=\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{N}\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}+\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{N}\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p})+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
&=N^{\kappa}\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}-\frac{N^{\kappa}\|\sigma\|_{2}^{2}}{N}\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+N^{\kappa}\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p})+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\end{split}

and, similarly,

\begin{split}\frac{N^{\kappa}N_{0}}{N}&\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p}^{2}=N^{\kappa}\sum_{p}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\end{split}

as well as

\sum_{p}p^{2}\sigma_{p}^{2}=\sum_{p}p^{2}\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).

Putting together all constant contributions, we find

\begin{split}&\frac{N^{1+\kappa}}{2}\hat{V}(0)+\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2}+N^{\kappa}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}-\frac{N^{\kappa}\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{2}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}\\
&+N^{\kappa}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p})+N^{\kappa}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2}\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}\eta_{\infty,p}\\
&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p})+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\\
\end{split}

(115)

With the scattering equation (
26
) and with (
27
), we combine

\begin{split}N^{\kappa}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}\eta_{\infty,p}\\
=&-\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\eta_{\infty,p}^{2}+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}+O(N^{2\kappa})\\
=&-\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\eta_{\infty,p}^{2}+\frac{8\pi\mathfrak{a}-\hat{V}(0)}{2}N^{1+\kappa}+O(N^{2\kappa})\end{split}

and, using also (
32
),

\begin{split}N^{\kappa}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p})&+\frac{N^{\kappa}}{N}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p})\\
&=-2\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\eta_{\infty,p}(\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-\eta_{\infty,p})+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Moreover, we write

\begin{split}N^{\kappa}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2}=&-2\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\eta_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2}\\
&-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p+r}\sigma_{p}^{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Therefore, (
115
) can be rewritten as

\begin{split}&4\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{1+\kappa}+\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}(\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2}+\eta_{\infty,p}^{2}-2\eta_{\infty,p}\gamma_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}-2\eta_{\infty,p}\sigma_{\infty,p}^{2})\\
&-\frac{N^{\kappa}\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{2}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{p+r}\sigma_{p}^{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}+N^{2\kappa})\end{split}

and thus, inserting (
25
), as

\begin{split}&4\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{1+\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\left((\frac{1-2\eta_{\infty,p}}{\sqrt{1-4\eta_{\infty,p}}}-1)(1-2\eta_{\infty,p})+2\eta_{\infty,p}^{2}-4\frac{\eta_{\infty,p}^{2}}{\sqrt{1-4\eta_{\infty,p}}}\right)\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt-\frac{N^{\kappa}\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{2}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{p+r}\sigma_{p}^{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}+N^{2\kappa})\\
&=4\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{1+\kappa}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\left(\sqrt{1-4\eta_{\infty,p}}-1+2\eta_{\infty,p}+2\eta_{\infty,p}^{2}\right)\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt-\frac{N^{\kappa}\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{2}}{N}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p}-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{p+r}\sigma_{p}^{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}+N^{2\kappa}).\end{split}

With the definition (
23
) of
\eta_{\infty,p}
, we obtain exactly the constant term in (
106
).
∎

3.2
Bounds for negligible cubic expectations

In this subsection, we estimate the expectations
\langle\xi,\mathcal{Q}\xi\rangle,\langle\xi,(\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}^{*})\xi\rangle,\langle\xi,\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}\xi\rangle
, appearing on the r.h.s. of (
106
), and we show that they are at most of order
N^{4\kappa-1}
and therefore negligible for our purposes. We will make use of the following preliminary lemma to bound expectations of cubic operators.

Lemma 14
.

Let

\mathcal{C}_{K}=\int dy_{1}dy_{2}dy_{3}\,K(y_{1}-y_{2},y_{1}-y_{3})a_{y_{1}}a_{y_{2}}a_{y_{3}}+\text{h.c.}\

(116)

with
K\in L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
. We assume that
K(y,z)=\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}K_{u,v}(y,z)
,
where
K_{u,v}(y,z)=0
, if
|y-u|>10\ell_{B}
or
|z-v|>10\ell_{B}
. Then, for every
\epsilon>0
and
\nu>0
, we have

\begin{split}\big|\langle&e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{C}_{K}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\\
&\leq|\langle\Omega,[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}]\Omega\rangle|\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt+C\ell_{B}^{3}N^{(3\kappa-1)/2+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert K_{u,v}\rVert_{2}\big)\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{N}e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle+CN^{-\nu}\end{split}

(117)

for all
t\in[0;1]
, if we choose
\alpha>0
and then
n
large enough. Notice that, in (
117
), we can bound

|\langle\Omega,[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}]\Omega\rangle|\leq C\|A\|_{2}\|K\|_{2}\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2}\|K\|_{2}.

Proof.

We take
t=1
(the extension to
t\in[0;1]
is straightforward). With Duhamel, we obtain

\begin{split}&\big|\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{C}_{K}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\\
&\leq\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\big|\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}\Theta]e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\\
&\leq\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\big|\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,(\Theta[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}]\Theta+(1-\Theta)[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}]\Theta\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt+A^{*}\mathcal{C}_{K}(\Theta-1)-A^{*}(\Theta-1)\mathcal{C}_{K})e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\\
&\leq\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\big|\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\Theta[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}]\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|+CN^{-\nu}\end{split}

(118)

if
\alpha>0
and then
n\in\mathbb{N}
are chosen sufficiently large in (
91
). Here we estimated all terms containing a factor
\Theta-1
with Lemma
11
, in particular (
95
), (
96
) and (
97
), using also the bound
\|A^{*}(\mathcal{N}+1)^{-3/2}\|\leq C\|A\|_{2},\|\mathcal{C}_{K}(\mathcal{N}+1)^{-3/2}\|\leq C\lVert K\rVert_{2}
.

We observe that

\begin{split}[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}]=\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}&dy_{1}dy_{2}dy_{3}\,K(y_{1}-y_{2},y_{1}-y_{3})\\
&\times\check{A}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x_{3})\big[a_{y_{1}}a_{y_{2}}a_{y_{3}},a_{x_{1}}^{*}a_{x_{2}}^{*}a_{x_{3}}^{*}\big]\end{split}

(119)

where

\begin{split}\big[a_{y_{1}}a_{y_{2}}a_{y_{3}},a_{x_{1}}^{*}a_{x_{2}}^{*}a_{x_{3}}^{*}\big]=\;&\sum_{\pi\in S_{3}}\delta(y_{1}-x_{\pi 1})\delta(y_{2}-x_{\pi 2})\delta(y_{3}-x_{\pi 3})\\
&+\sum_{i_{1}<i_{2}}^{3}\sum_{\pi\in S_{3}}\delta(y_{i_{1}}-x_{\pi i_{1}})\delta(y_{i_{2}}-x_{\pi i_{2}})\prod_{i_{3}\not=i_{1},i_{2}}a_{x_{\pi i_{3}}}^{*}a_{y_{i_{3}}}\\
&+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}\delta(y_{i}-x_{j})\prod_{\ell\not=j}a^{*}_{x_{\ell}}\prod_{m\not=i}a_{y_{m}}.\end{split}

(120)

We decompose the main term on the r.h.s. of (
118
) in the sum
T_{4}+T_{2}+T_{0}
, where
T_{4}
are the contributions arising from the quartic terms on the r.h.s. of (
120
),
T_{2}
from the quadratic terms and
T_{0}=\langle\Omega,[\mathcal{C}_{K},A^{*}]\Omega\rangle
from the constant terms. It is easy to check that
|T_{0}|\leq C\|K\|_{2}\|A\|_{2}
. Let us focus on the term
T_{2}
. As an example, we consider the contribution
T_{2,0}
associated with
i_{1}=\pi i_{1}=1,i_{2}=\pi i_{2}=2
in (
120
). Localizing the kernels
K,\check{A}
, we can bound

\begin{split}|T_{2,0}|&\leq\sum_{u,v,\tilde{u},\tilde{v}\in\Lambda_{B}}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dy_{3}\,|K_{u,v}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-y_{3})||\check{A}_{\tilde{u},\tilde{v}}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x_{3})|\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times\big|\langle\Theta e^{sA^{*}-\text{h.c.}}\Omega,a_{x_{3}}^{*}a_{y_{3}}\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}}\Omega\rangle\big|\\
&\leq\sum_{u,v,\tilde{u},\tilde{v}\in\Lambda_{B}}\int_{0}^{1}ds\Big(\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dy_{3}\,|K_{u,v}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-y_{3})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 71.13188pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-x_{2}-\tilde{u}|,|x_{1}-x_{3}-\tilde{v}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{x_{3}}\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\times\Big(\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dy_{3}\,|\check{A}_{\tilde{u},\tilde{v}}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x_{3})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 71.13188pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-x_{2}-u|,|x_{1}-y_{3}-v|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{y_{3}}\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}.\end{split}

Using the characteristic functions to gain a volume factor
\ell_{B}^{3}
(after switching to variables
x_{2}^{\prime}=x_{1}-x_{2}
,
y^{\prime}_{3}=y_{3}-x_{1}
,
x^{\prime}_{1}=x_{1}-x_{3}
and
x^{\prime}_{3}=x_{3}
in the first integral on the r.h.s. and to variables
x^{\prime}_{2}=x_{2}-x_{1}
,
x^{\prime}_{3}=x_{3}-x_{1}
,
x^{\prime}_{1}=x_{1}-y_{3}
,
y^{\prime}_{3}=y_{3}
in the second) and applying Lemma
8
, we can estimate

|T_{2,0}|\leq C\ell_{B}^{3}N^{(3\kappa-1)/2+\epsilon}\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\|K_{u,v}\|_{2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{N}e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle.

The other contributions to
T_{2}
can be bounded similarly. Let us now consider
T_{4}
. Also here, we focus on an example, taking the term
T_{4,0}
associated with
i=j=1
on the third line of (
120
). We find

\begin{split}|T_{4,0}|&\leq\sum_{u,v,\tilde{u},\tilde{v}\in\Lambda_{B}}\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dy_{2}dy_{3}|K_{u,v}(x_{1}-y_{2},x_{1}-y_{3})|\,|\check{A}_{\tilde{u},\tilde{v}}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x_{3})|\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\int_{0}^{1}ds\,|\langle\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,a^{*}_{x_{2}}a^{*}_{x_{3}}a_{y_{2}}a_{y_{3}}\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle|\\
&\leq\sum_{u,v,\tilde{u},\tilde{v}\in\Lambda_{B}}\int_{0}^{1}ds\Big(\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dy_{2}dy_{3}\,|K_{u,v}(x_{1}-y_{2},x_{1}-y_{3})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-x_{2}-\tilde{u}|,|x_{1}-x_{3}-\tilde{v}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{x_{2}}a_{x_{3}}\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\times\Big(\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dy_{2}dy_{3}\,|\check{A}_{\tilde{u},\tilde{v}}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x_{3})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-y_{2}-u|,|x_{1}-y_{3}-v|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{y_{2}}a_{y_{3}}\Theta e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}.\end{split}

(121)

We apply Lemma
11
to estimate, with the notation
\xi_{s}=e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
,

\begin{split}\int\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-\tilde{v}-x_{3}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}dx_{3}=\|\mathcal{N}_{10\ell_{B}}^{1/2}(x_{1}-\tilde{v})a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|\leq CN^{\epsilon}\|a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|\\
\int\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-v-y_{3}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{y_{3}}a_{y_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}dy_{3}=\|\mathcal{N}_{10\ell_{B}}^{1/2}(x_{1}-v)a_{y_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|\leq CN^{\epsilon}\|a_{y_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|\end{split}

if
n
is large enough. With Lemma
8
, we conclude that

|T_{4,0}|\leq C\ell_{B}^{3}N^{(3\kappa-1)/2+\epsilon}\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\|K_{u,v}\|_{2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{N}e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle.

Also the other contributions to
T_{4}
can be handled similarly.
∎

An immediate application of Lemma
14
is the following bound for the expectation of the number of excitations generated by the cubic transformation.

Lemma 15
.

We have

\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{N}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}

(122)

for all
t\in[0;1]
, provided in (
91
) we choose
\alpha
and then
n
large enough.

Proof.

With
[\mathcal{N},A^{*}]=3A^{*}
, we find

\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{N}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle=2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,(3A^{*}+3A^{*}(\Theta-1))e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle.

The term proportional to
\Theta-1
can be handled with Lemma
11
; it can be made arbitrarily small, choosing
\alpha>0
and then
n\in\mathbb{N}
large enough in (
91
). To estimate the expectation of
A^{*}
, we apply Lemma
14
, with
K=A
. From Lemma
7
and Lemma
8
, we obtain

\begin{split}\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }&\Omega,\mathcal{N}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}+CN^{-\beta/4+\epsilon}\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{N}e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\end{split}

for any
\epsilon>0
, if
\alpha,n
are large enough. Choosing
\epsilon>0
small enough, we can apply Gronwall’s lemma to show (
122
).
∎

Inserting the bound (
122
) back into (
117
), we obtain the following corollary.

Corollary 16
.

Suppose the operator
\mathcal{C}_{K}
is defined as in Lemma
14
. For every
\epsilon>0
, we have

\begin{split}\big|&\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{C}_{K}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2}\Big(\lVert K\rVert_{2}+N^{-\beta/4+\epsilon}\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert K_{u,v}\rVert_{2}\Big)\end{split}

(123)

for all
t\in[0;1]
, provided
\alpha
and then
n
are large enough.

We will also need bounds similar to the one in Lemma
14
for cubic operators with slightly more general kernels.

Corollary 17
.

For
f\in L^{1}(\Lambda)
and a kernel
K
as in Lemma
14
, let

\mathcal{C}_{K,f}=\int dydy_{1}dy_{2}dy_{3}\,f(y)K(y+y_{1}-y_{2},y+y_{1}-y_{3})a_{y_{1}}^{*}a_{y_{2}}^{*}a_{y_{3}}^{*}+\text{h.c.}\ .

Then we have, for any fixed
\epsilon>0
,

\begin{split}\big|\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,&\mathcal{C}_{K,f}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\\
&\leq|\langle\Omega,[\mathcal{C}_{K,f},A^{*}]\Omega\rangle|+C\|f\|_{1}N^{(3\kappa-1)/2-\beta/4+\epsilon}\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert K_{u,v}\rVert_{2}\end{split}

for all
t\in[0;1]
, provided
\alpha
and then
n
are large enough.

Proof.

We proceed as in the proof of Lemma
14
. To bound contributions to
[\mathcal{C}_{K,f},A^{*}]
that are quadratic or quartic in creation and annihilation operators, we write
\mathcal{C}_{K,f}
as average with weight
f
of operators that have exactly the form considered in Lemma
14
. This observation allows us to derive the desired bound.
∎

The last two corollaries allow us to control the expectation
\langle\xi,(\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}^{*})\xi\rangle
appearing on the r.h.s. of (
106
).

Lemma 18
.

From (
106
) recall the definition

\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})((\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{r}-1)\sigma_{p}+\sigma_{p+r}\sigma_{r}\gamma_{p})a_{p+r}a_{-r}a_{-p}.

We have

\big|\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}

(124)

for
\alpha
and
n
chosen large enough.

Proof.

We decompose
\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(j)}_{N}
, where

\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(j)}_{N}=\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}K^{(j)}_{r,p}a_{p+r}a_{-r}a_{-p}

with the coefficients

\begin{split}K^{(1)}_{r,p}&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{r}-1)\sigma_{p}\\
K^{(2)}_{r,p}&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\gamma_{r}(\gamma_{p+r}-1)\sigma_{p}\\
K^{(3)}_{r,p}&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p+r}\sigma_{r}\\
K^{(4)}_{r,p}&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{p+r}\sigma_{r}(\gamma_{p}-1).\end{split}

With the momentum space bounds from Lemma
5
, we get
\|K^{(j)}\|_{2}\leq CN^{(5\kappa-1)/2}
, for all
j=1,\dots,4
. Switching to position space, we write, for
j=1,\dots,4
,

\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(j)}_{N}=\int dxdydz\,\check{K}^{(j)}(x-y,x-z)a_{x}a_{y}a_{z}

with

\begin{split}\check{K}^{(1)}(y,z)&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}[N^{3-3\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}\cdot)*(\widecheck{\gamma-1})](y)\check{\sigma}(z)\\
\check{K}^{(2)}(y,z)&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\int dw\,[N^{3-3\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}\cdot)*\check{\gamma}](w)(\widecheck{\gamma-1})(w+y)\check{\sigma}(w+z)\\
\check{K}^{(3)}(y,z)&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}[N^{3-3\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}\cdot)*\check{\sigma}](y)\check{\sigma}(z)\\
\check{K}^{(4)}(y,z)&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\int dw\,[N^{3-3\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}\cdot)*\check{\sigma}](w)\check{\sigma}(w+y)(\widecheck{\gamma-1})(w+z).\end{split}

For
u\in\Lambda_{B}=\Lambda\cap\ell_{B}\mathbb{Z}^{3}
, we recall
\check{\sigma}_{u}=\check{\sigma}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}
and
\widecheck{(\gamma-1)}_{u}=\widecheck{(\gamma-1)}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}
, with
B_{u}=\{x\in\Lambda,\inf_{v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lvert x-v\rvert=\lvert x-u\rvert\}
. For
u,v\in\Lambda_{B}
, we define the localized kernel

\begin{split}K^{(1)}_{u,v}(y,z)&=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}[N^{3-3\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}\cdot)*\widecheck{(\gamma-1)}_{u}](y)\check{\sigma}_{v}(z)\end{split}

and we notice that
\|K^{(1)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\leq CN^{\kappa-1/2}\|\widecheck{(\gamma-1)}_{u}\|_{2}\|\check{\sigma}_{v}\|_{2}
. With Lemma
6
we find

\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\|K^{(1)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\leq CN^{(5\kappa-1)/2+\epsilon}

for any
\epsilon>0
, if
\alpha
and then
n
are large enough. Recalling that
\|K^{(1)}\|_{2}\leq CN^{(5\kappa-1)/2}
, we obtain from Corollary
16
that

\big|\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(1)}_{N}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}(1+N^{-\beta/4+\epsilon})\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}

if
\epsilon>0
is chosen small enough (and
\alpha
and then
n
are large enough).
The expectation of
\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(3)}_{N}
can be bounded analogously. To control the contribution of
\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(2)}_{N}
, we observe that

\check{K}^{(2)}(y,z)=\int dw\,f(w)\tilde{K}^{(2)}(w+y,w+z)

with
f(w)=[N^{3-3\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}\cdot)*\check{\gamma}](w)
and
\tilde{K}^{(2)}(y,z)=N^{\kappa-1}N_{0}^{1/2}\widecheck{(\gamma-1)}(y)\check{\sigma}(z)
. From Lemma
6
, we have

\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\|\tilde{K}^{(2)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\leq N^{5\kappa/2-1/2+\epsilon}.

Since moreover

|\langle\Omega,[\tilde{\mathcal{C}}_{N}^{(2)},A^{*}]\Omega\rangle|\leq C\|K^{(2)}\|_{2}\|A\|_{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}

and
\|f\|_{1}\leq CN^{\beta/8+\epsilon}
(with the bounds in Lemma
5
, we find
\|\widecheck{\gamma-1}\|_{1}\leq CN^{\beta/8+\epsilon}
, for any
\epsilon>0
), Corollary
17
implies that

\big|\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(2)}_{N}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}(1+N^{-\beta/8+\epsilon})\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}

if
\epsilon>0
is chosen sufficiently small. The expectation of
\tilde{\mathcal{C}}^{(4)}_{N}
can be handled analogously.
∎

In the next lemma, we show a bound for the expectation of the operator
\tilde{\mathcal{N}}=\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}}\sigma_{p}^{2}a_{p}^{*}a_{p}
. Since it will be useful in the next sections, we actually prove a more general estimate, controlling the expectation of general non-negative quadratic operators, diagonal in momentum space. For
k\in L^{1}(\Lambda)
, we introduce the notation

\mathcal{N}_{k}=\int dzdz^{\prime}\,k(z-z^{\prime})a_{z}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}\,.

Lemma 19
.

Let
h\in L^{2}(\Lambda)
, real-valued. We assume that
h(x)=\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}h_{u}(x)
, where
h_{u}\in L^{2}(\Lambda)
for all
u\in\Lambda_{B}
, with
h_{u}(x)=0
, if
|x-u|>10\ell_{B}
.
Then, for every
\epsilon>0
there is a constant
C>0
such that

\begin{split}\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,&\mathcal{N}_{h\star h}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&\leq C\langle\Omega,\big[A,\big[\mathcal{N}_{h\star h},A^{*}\big]\big]\Omega\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\Big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\Big)^{2}\end{split}

(125)

for all
t\in[0;1]
, if
\alpha
and then
n\in\mathbb{N}
are large enough. Here, we used the notation

h\star h(x)=\int h(y-x)h(y)dy

(
h\star h
is the usual convolution, if
h(-y)=h(y)
). In particular, recalling that
\tilde{\mathcal{N}}=\mathcal{N}_{\check{\sigma}\star\check{\sigma}}
, we find
C>0
such that, for all
t\in[0;1]
,

\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\tilde{\mathcal{N}}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}.

Remark.
For
k\in L^{1}(\Lambda)
, we also define the operator

\ddot{\mathcal{N}}_{k}=\int dzdz^{\prime}\,k(z-z^{\prime})[a_{z^{\prime}},A^{*}][a_{z}^{*},-A]

(126)

contributing to the second derivative of
\mathcal{N}_{k}
(hence the notation). From the proof of Lemma
19
, we also obtain

\big|\langle e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\ddot{\mathcal{N}}_{h\star h}e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\big|\leq CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|\big)^{2}

for all
s\in[0;1]
and for any
\epsilon>0
, if
\alpha
and then
n
are chosen large enough in the definition of the cutoff
\Theta
. This bound will be used in the next subsection.

Proof.

Throughout the proof, we will use the notation
g=h\star h
. By Cauchy-Schwarz, we have

\|h\|_{1}=\int|h(x)|dx\leq\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\int|h_{u}(x)|dx\leq C\ell_{B}^{3/2}\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}

and thus

\begin{split}\|\hat{h}\|_{\infty}\leq C\ell_{B}^{3/2}\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2},\qquad\|\hat{g}\|_{\infty}\leq\|g\|_{1}\leq C\ell_{B}^{3}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}.\end{split}

Setting
\xi_{t}=e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
for
t\in[0;1]
, we find

\begin{split}&\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{N}_{g}\xi_{t}\rangle=2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\big([\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}]+[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}](\Theta-1)+A^{*}[\mathcal{N}_{g},(\Theta-1)]\big)\xi_{s}\rangle.\end{split}

All terms containing a factor
(\Theta-1)
can be handled with Lemma
11
, in particular with (
95
), (
96
), (
97
). Since
\mathcal{N}_{g}\leq\|\hat{g}\|_{\infty}\mathcal{N}
,
\|A(\mathcal{N}+1)^{-3/2}\|\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2}
, it is easy to check that they can be included in the error proportional to
(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2})^{2}
on the r.h.s. of (
125
), if we choose
\alpha>0
and then
n\in\mathbb{N}
large enough in (
91
). Also in the rest of the proof we will ignore these terms. With the definition (
75
), we compute

[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}]=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}\,g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{x_{1}}a^{*}_{x_{2}}.

(127)

Expanding once more, we arrive at

\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{N}_{g}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq 2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds_{1}\int_{0}^{s_{1}}ds_{2}\,\langle\xi_{s_{2}},[[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}],-A]\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}

where

[[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}],-A]=\langle\Omega,[A,[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle+T_{2}+T_{4,1}+T_{4,2}

(128)

with

\begin{split}T_{2}&=-\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}\,g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{3}-x_{2},x_{3}-z)a^{*}_{x_{1}}a_{x_{3}}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}\,g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{3}-x_{2},x_{3}-x_{1})a^{*}_{z}a_{x_{3}}\end{split}

and

\begin{split}T_{4,1}&=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,g(z-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-x_{1})a^{*}_{z}a^{*}_{x_{2}}a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\\
T_{4,2}&=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,g(z-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)a^{*}_{x_{1}}a^{*}_{x_{2}}a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\end{split}

where we recall the kernels
\check{A}^{(3)},\check{A}^{(6)}
defined in (
75
).
Using (
78
), we pass to momentum space, writing

T_{2}=\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{g}_{p}\,A^{(6)}_{q,p}\,A^{(3)}_{q,p}\big(a_{p}^{*}a_{p}+a_{q}^{*}a_{q}+a_{-p-q}^{*}a_{-p-q}\big).

With (
78
) and with Lemma
9
, we estimate

\begin{split}\big|\langle\xi_{s_{2}},T_{2}\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle\big|&\leq CN^{3\kappa/2-1}\|\hat{g}\|_{\infty}\langle\xi_{s_{2}},(\mathcal{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{N}})\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle\\
&\leq CN^{\beta/4-1}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\langle\xi_{s_{2}},(\mathcal{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{N}})\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle.\end{split}

We now consider the term
T_{4,1}
. Using Lemma
11
to insert the cutoff
\Theta
(choosing
\alpha>0
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
large enough), it is enough to estimate

\begin{split}&\lvert\langle\xi_{s_{2}},\Theta T_{4,1}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle\rvert\\
&\leq\int dydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\lvert h(y-z^{\prime})\rvert\lvert\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\rvert\lvert\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-x_{1})\rvert\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\Big|\int dz\,h(y-z)\langle\Theta\xi_{s_{2}},a^{*}_{z}a_{x_{2}}^{*}a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle\Big|.\end{split}

Next, we localize
h
and the kernels
\check{A}^{(3)},\check{A}^{(6)}
. With Cauchy-Schwarz, we obtain

\begin{split}&\lvert\langle\xi_{s_{2}},\Theta T_{4,1}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle\rvert\\
&\leq\sum_{u,v,\tilde{u},\tilde{v},w\in\Lambda_{B}}\Big(\int dydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|h_{w}(y-z^{\prime})|^{2}\lvert\check{A}^{(3)}_{\tilde{u},\tilde{v}}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-x_{1})\rvert^{2}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\mathbbm{1}(\lvert x_{1}-x_{2}-u\rvert,\lvert x_{1}-z^{\prime}-v\rvert\leq 10\ell_{B})\Big\|\int dz\,h(y-z)a_{x_{2}}a_{z}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\Big\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Big(\int dydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\lvert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\rvert^{2}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\mathbbm{1}(\lvert x_{3}-x_{4}-\tilde{u}\rvert,\lvert x_{3}-x_{1}-\tilde{v}\rvert,\lvert y-z^{\prime}-w\rvert\leq 10\ell_{B})\lVert a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\rVert^{2}\Big)^{1/2}.\end{split}

Applying Lemma
11
and then Lemma
9
we find, for any
\epsilon>0
,

\begin{split}\lvert\langle\xi_{s_{2}},&\Theta T_{4,1}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle\rvert\\
&\leq C\ell_{B}^{9/2}N^{\epsilon/2}\big(\sum_{w\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert h_{w}\rVert_{2}\big)\big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}\rVert_{2}\big)\big(\sum_{\tilde{u},\tilde{v}\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{A}^{(3)}_{\tilde{u},\tilde{v}}\rVert_{2}\big)\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\|\Big(\int dy\;\Big\|\int dz\,h(y-z)a_{z}\Theta\xi_{s_{2}}\Big\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\leq CN^{\beta/4-1+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\langle\xi_{s_{2}},\mathcal{N}\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle+C\langle\xi_{s_{2}},\mathcal{N}_{g}\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\end{split}

if the parameters
\alpha>0
and
n
in the definition of
\Theta
are chosen sufficiently large.

To bound
T_{4,2}
, we expand it once again, estimating

\begin{split}\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}&ds_{1}\int_{0}^{s_{1}}ds_{2}\,\langle\xi_{s_{2}},T_{4,2}\xi_{s_{2}}\rangle\\
&\leq\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds_{1}\int_{0}^{s_{1}}ds_{2}\int_{0}^{s_{2}}ds_{3}\,\langle\xi_{s_{3}},\Theta[T_{4,2},A^{*}]\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\end{split}

for
\alpha>0
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
large enough (using again Lemma
11
to insert
\Theta
). We compute

[T_{4,2},A^{*}]=T_{4,2,3}+T_{4,2,5}

with

\begin{split}T_{4,2,3}&=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}dx_{5}\,g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{5}-x_{3},x_{5}-x_{4})a^{*}_{x_{1}}a^{*}_{x_{2}}a^{*}_{x_{5}}\\
T_{4,2,5}&=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}dx_{5}dx_{6}\,g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)A^{(3)}(x_{5}-x_{6},x_{5}-x_{4})a^{*}_{x_{1}}a^{*}_{x_{2}}a^{*}_{x_{5}}a^{*}_{x_{6}}a_{x_{3}}.\end{split}

For the quintic term, we find

\begin{split}\lvert\langle&\xi_{s_{3}},\Theta T_{4,2,5}\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rangle\rvert\\
&\leq\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}dx_{5}dx_{6}\,\lvert g(z-z^{\prime})\rvert\lvert\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\rvert\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\times\lvert\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\rvert\lvert\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{5}-x_{6},x_{5}-x_{4})\rvert\lVert a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}a_{x_{5}}a_{x_{6}}\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rVert\lVert a_{x_{3}}\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rVert.\end{split}

(129)

Localizing the kernels
\check{A}^{(3)},\check{A}^{(6)}
and applying Cauchy-Schwarz, Lemma
11
and Lemma
9
, we obtain

\begin{split}\lvert\langle&\xi_{s_{3}},\Theta T_{4,2,5}\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rangle\rvert\\
&\leq\sum_{u_{i},v_{i}\in\Lambda_{B}:\,i=1,2,3}\Big(\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}dx_{5}dx_{6}\,\lvert g(z-z^{\prime})\rvert\lvert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u_{2},v_{2}}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\rvert^{2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-x_{2}-u_{1}|,|x_{1}-z^{\prime}-v_{1}|,|x_{5}-x_{6}-u_{3}|,|x_{5}-x_{4}-v_{3}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\lVert a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}a_{x_{5}}a_{x_{6}}\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rVert^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big(\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}dx_{5}dx_{6}\,\lvert g(z-z^{\prime})\rvert\lvert\check{A}^{(3)}_{u_{1},v_{1}}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\rvert^{2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\lvert\check{A}^{(3)}_{u_{3},v_{3}}(x_{5}-x_{6},x_{5}-x_{4})\rvert^{2}\mathbbm{1}(|x_{3}-x_{4}-u_{2}|,|x_{3}-z-v_{2}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\lVert a_{x_{3}}\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rVert^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\leq CN^{\epsilon}\Big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\ell_{B}^{3/2}\lVert\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}\rVert\Big)^{2}\Big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\ell_{B}^{3/2}\lVert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}\rVert\Big)\lVert g\rVert_{1}\langle\Theta\xi_{s_{3}},\mathcal{N}\Theta\xi_{s_{3}}\rangle\\
&\leq CN^{3\beta/8-1+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|\big)^{2}\,\langle\xi_{s_{3}},\mathcal{N}\xi_{s_{3}}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\end{split}

for
n\in\mathbb{N}
large enough. As for the cubic terms
T_{4,2,3}
, we need to expand it a last time, writing

\begin{split}&\text{Re }\int_{0}^{1}ds_{1}\int_{0}^{s_{1}}ds_{2}\int_{0}^{s_{2}}d{s_{3}}\,\langle\xi_{s_{3}},T_{4,2,3}\xi_{s_{3}}\rangle\\
&\leq\text{Re }\int_{0}^{1}ds_{1}\int_{0}^{s_{1}}ds_{2}\int_{0}^{s_{2}}d{s_{3}}\int_{0}^{s_{3}}ds_{4}\,\langle\xi_{s_{4}},\Theta[T_{4,2,3},A^{*}]\Theta\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}.\end{split}

A long but straightforward computation shows that

[T_{4,2,3},A^{*}]=T_{4,2,3,0}+T_{4,2,3,2}+T_{4,2,3,4}

with

\begin{split}T_{4,2,3,0}&=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}\dots dx_{5}\;g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{5}-x_{3},x_{5}-x_{4})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x_{5})\\
T_{4,2,3,2}&=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}\dots dx_{6}\;g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{5}-x_{3},x_{5}-x_{4})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{6}-x_{1},x_{6}-x_{2})a^{*}_{x_{5}}a_{x_{6}}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}\dots dx_{6}\;g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{5}-x_{3},x_{5}-x_{4})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{6}-x_{5},x_{6}-x_{2})a^{*}_{x_{1}}a_{x_{6}}\\
T_{4,2,3,4}&=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}\dots dx_{7}\;g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{5}-x_{3},x_{5}-x_{4})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{6}-x_{7},x_{6}-x_{1})a^{*}_{x_{5}}a^{*}_{x_{2}}a_{x_{6}}a_{x_{7}}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt+\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}\dots dx_{7}\;g(z-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-z)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{5}-x_{3},x_{5}-x_{4})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{6}-x_{7},x_{6}-x_{5})a^{*}_{x_{1}}a^{*}_{x_{2}}a_{x_{6}}a_{x_{7}}.\\
\end{split}

For
T_{4,2,3,0}
and
T_{4,2,3,2}
we switch to momentum space. With (
75
) and Lemma
9
, we obtain

\begin{split}\lvert T_{4,2,3,0}\rvert&\leq\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}|\hat{g}_{p}|\lvert A^{(6)}_{r,p}\rvert\lvert A^{(3)}_{r,p}\rvert\lvert A^{(6)}_{q,p}\rvert\lvert A^{(3)}_{q,p}\rvert\\
&\leq C\|\hat{g}\|_{\infty}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}|A^{(6)}_{r,p}|^{2}|A^{(3)}_{q,p}|^{2}\\
&\leq CN^{-\beta/2}\|\hat{g}\|_{\infty}\sum_{p,r\in\Lambda^{*}}(1+\sigma_{p}^{2})\lvert A^{(6)}_{r,p}\rvert^{2}\leq CN^{-3\beta/4+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\end{split}

for any
\epsilon>0
. Similarly,

\begin{split}|\langle\xi_{s_{4}}&,T_{4,2,3,2}\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle|\\
&\leq C\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}|\hat{g}_{p}|\lvert A^{(6)}_{r,p}\rvert\lvert A^{(3)}_{r,p}\rvert\lvert A^{(6)}_{q,p}\rvert\lvert A^{(3)}_{q,p}\rvert\langle\xi_{s_{4}},(a_{p}^{*}a_{p}+a_{q}^{*}a_{q}+a_{p+q}^{*}a_{p+q})\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle\\
&\leq CN^{-\beta/2}\lVert\hat{g}\rVert_{\infty}(1+\|\sigma\|_{\infty}^{2})\sum_{j=3,6}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\lvert A^{(j)}_{q,p}\rvert^{2}\langle\xi_{s_{4}},(a_{p}^{*}a_{p}+a_{q}^{*}a_{q}+a_{p+q}^{*}a_{p+q})\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle\\
&\leq CN^{-1+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\langle\xi_{s_{4}},(\mathcal{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{N}})\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle.\end{split}

As for the quartic contribution, we find that, for any
\epsilon>0
,

\begin{split}\lvert\langle\Theta\xi_{s_{4}},T_{4,2,3,4}\Theta\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle\rvert&\leq CN^{\epsilon}\Big(\big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\ell_{B}^{3/2}\lVert\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}\rVert\big)^{4}+\big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\ell_{B}^{3/2}\lVert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}\rVert\big)^{4}\Big)\lVert g\rVert_{1}\langle\xi_{s_{4}},\mathcal{N}\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle\\
&\leq CN^{\beta/4-1+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\langle\xi_{s_{4}},\mathcal{N}\xi_{s_{4}}\rangle,\end{split}

if
n\in\mathbb{N}
in the definition of
\Theta
is large enough. This bound can be shown proceeding similarly as we did for
T_{4,2,5}
in (
129
); we omit the details.

Combining all the bounds we proved, we obtain

\begin{split}\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{N}_{g}\,\xi_{t}\rangle\leq\;&\text{Re }\langle\Omega,[A,[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle+CN^{3\beta/8-1+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|\big)^{2}\,\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},(\mathcal{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{N}})\xi_{s}\rangle\\
&+C\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{N}_{g}\xi_{s}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}\end{split}

for all
t\in[0;1]
. With Lemma
15
, we arrive at

\begin{split}\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{N}_{g}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq\;&\text{Re }\langle\Omega,[A,[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle+CN^{3\beta/8-1+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|\big)^{2}\,\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\tilde{\mathcal{N}}\xi_{s}\rangle\\
&+C\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{N}_{g}\xi_{s}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}.\end{split}

(130)

Consider first the choice
h=\check{\sigma}
. By Lemma
6
, we have
\sum_{u}\|\check{\sigma}_{u}\|_{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa/4+\epsilon}
, for any
\epsilon>0
. Since
\tilde{\mathcal{N}}=\mathcal{N}_{\check{\sigma}*\check{\sigma}}
, (
130
) implies that

\begin{split}\langle\xi_{t},\tilde{\mathcal{N}}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq\;&\text{Re }\langle\Omega,[A,[\tilde{\mathcal{N}},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle+C(1+N^{-\beta/8+\epsilon})\,\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\tilde{\mathcal{N}}\xi_{s}\rangle+CN^{3\kappa-1-\beta/8+\epsilon}\end{split}

Here, we have

\langle\Omega,[A,[\tilde{\mathcal{N}},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle=\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}\,(\check{\sigma}*\check{\sigma})(z-z^{\prime})A^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})A^{(6)}(z-x_{1},z-x_{2})

and thus, passing to momentum space and using Lemma
9
,

\big|\langle\Omega,[A,[\tilde{\mathcal{N}},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle\big|=\Big|\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\sigma_{q}^{2}A^{(3)}_{p,q}A^{(6)}_{p,q}\Big|\leq\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\sigma_{q}^{2}|A^{(3)}_{p,q}||A^{(6)}_{p,q}|\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}.

Hence, choosing
\epsilon>0
small enough, we find

\begin{split}\langle\xi_{t},\tilde{\mathcal{N}}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq\;&CN^{3\kappa-1}+C\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\tilde{\mathcal{N}}\xi_{s}\rangle.\end{split}

By Gronwall, we conclude that
\langle\xi_{t},\tilde{\mathcal{N}}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}

for all
t\in[0;1]
.

Inserting this bound back on the r.h.s. of (
130
) we find, now for a general
h\in L^{2}(\Lambda)
,

\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{N}_{h\star h}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq\;\text{Re }\langle\Omega,[A,[\mathcal{N}_{h\star h},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle+C\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{N}_{g}\xi_{s}\rangle+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}.

Thus, again by Gronwall’s lemma, we conclude that

\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{N}_{h*h}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq C|\langle\Omega,[A,[\mathcal{N}_{h*h},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle|+CN^{-5\beta/8+\epsilon}\big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|h_{u}\|_{2}\big)^{2}

for all
t\in[0;1]
.

The remark after the lemma follows by the remark that the operator
\ddot{\mathcal{N}}_{h\star h}
coincides exactly with the term
T_{4,2}
, introduced in (
128
).
∎

Finally, we estimate the expectation
\langle\xi,\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}\xi\rangle
, with
\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}
as defined in (
107
).

Lemma 20
.

We have

\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}

if the parameters
\alpha
and then
n
in the definition (
89
) of the cutoff
\Theta
are large enough.

Proof.

We are going to bound the expectation of

\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}^{(0)}=\frac{N^{\kappa}}{2N}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{q-r}\gamma_{q}\gamma_{p}-1)a_{p+r}^{*}a_{q-r}^{*}a_{q}a_{p}.

All other contributions to
\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}
can be controlled analogously, replacing factors of
\gamma-1
by factors of
\sigma
. To estimate
\langle\xi,\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}^{(0)}\xi\rangle
, we observe that, setting
\theta_{p}=\gamma_{p}-1
, we have

\begin{split}\gamma_{p+r}\gamma_{q-r}\gamma_{q}\gamma_{p}-1=\;&\theta_{p+r}\theta_{q-r}\theta_{q}\theta_{p}+\theta_{p+r}\theta_{q-r}\theta_{q}+\theta_{p+r}\theta_{q-r}\theta_{p}+\theta_{p+r}\theta_{q}\theta_{p}+\theta_{q-r}\theta_{q}\theta_{p}\\
&+\theta_{p+r}\theta_{q-r}+\theta_{p+r}\theta_{q}+\theta_{p+r}\theta_{p}+\theta_{q-r}\theta_{q}+\theta_{q-r}\theta_{p}+\theta_{q}\theta_{p}\\
&+\theta_{p+r}+\theta_{q-r}+\theta_{q}+\theta_{p}.\end{split}

Next, we switch to position space. Setting
V_{N}(x)=N^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)
, we find

\begin{split}\tilde{\mathcal{V}}^{(0)}_{N}=\;&\frac{1}{2}\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{x})a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y})a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\\
&+\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,a^{*}_{x}a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y})a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})+\text{h.c.}\\
&+\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,a^{*}_{x}a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y})a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})\\
&+\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,a^{*}_{x}a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y})a(\check{\theta}_{x})a_{y}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})+\text{h.c.}\\
&+\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})+\text{h.c.}\end{split}

(131)

where we used the notation
\check{\theta}_{x}(s)=\check{\theta}(x-s)
, for
x\in\Lambda
. When estimating the expectation of all these terms, we can insert, on both sides of the inner products, the cutoff
\Theta
. For the first four terms (the ones without
V_{N}(x-y)a_{x}a_{y}
factor), this is done as before. As an example, we now show how to insert
\Theta
for the last term in (
131
) which has to be handled differently (even though we need to use Duhamel for showing that it is negligible).

\begin{split}\int&dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle\xi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi\rangle\\
=\;&\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle\Theta\xi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\rangle+\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle\xi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})(1-\Theta)\xi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle(1-\Theta)\xi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\rangle.\end{split}

(132)

The second (and analogously the third) term on the r.h.s. can be bounded by

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle\xi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})(1-\Theta)\xi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&\Big(\int dxdy\|a_{x}a_{y}(\mathcal{N}+1)\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\Big(\int dxdy\,V_{N}^{2}(x-y)\|a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})(\mathcal{N}+1)^{-1}(1-\Theta)\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&\sup_{y}\|\check{\theta}_{y}\|_{2}\|V_{N}\|_{2}\|(\mathcal{N}+1)^{2}\xi\|\|(1-\Theta)\xi\|.\end{split}

With Lemma
11
, we conclude that, for any
\nu>0
,

\Big|\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle\xi,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})(1-\Theta)\xi\rangle\Big|\leq CN^{-\nu}

if
\alpha>0
and then
n\in\mathbb{N}
are chosen large enough in (
91
).
Hence, we are left only with the first term on the r.h.s. of (
132
), where
\xi
has been replaced by
\Theta\xi
.

In Lemma
21
below, we are going to show that

\begin{split}\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\|^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/4+\epsilon}\\
\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\|^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/2+\epsilon}.\end{split}

(133)

With these bounds and with Cauchy-Schwarz, we can estimate the expectation of the first 4 terms on the r.h.s. of (
131
).

Let us consider the fifth term on the r.h.s. of (
131
). Recalling the definition (
75
) of the kernels
\check{A}^{(3)},\check{A}^{(6)}
, we find

[a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y},-A]=\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)+\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)

with

\begin{split}\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)&=\int dx_{1}dx_{2}\,\Big(\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x)a^{*}_{y}a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}+\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-y)a^{*}_{x}a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}\Big),\\
\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)&=\int dz\check{A}^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)a_{z}\,.\end{split}

(134)

Expanding
e^{-A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }
we find

\begin{split}\int dxdy\,&V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\xi,a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi\rangle\\
=\;&\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi\rangle+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\end{split}

with an arbitrarily small error
\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}
. In the second term, we expand the action of
e^{(1-s)A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }
on
\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)
. We find

\begin{split}\int dx&dy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\xi,a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi\rangle\\
=\;&\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\rangle\\
&+\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\xi,\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle\Theta\xi_{1-t},[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),A^{*}]e^{-tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\rangle\\
&+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}.\end{split}

Finally, in the second term we expand the action of
e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }
on
\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})
. We arrive at

\begin{split}\int dx&dy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\xi,a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi\rangle\\
=\;&\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\langle\Theta\xi_{1-t},[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),A^{*}]e^{-tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\Theta\xi_{s},[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),A^{*}]a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\Theta\xi_{s},[a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y}),A^{*}]\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\big[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),[a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y}),A^{*}]\big]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}.\end{split}

(135)

In Lemma
22
, we will prove that

\begin{split}\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{3}^{*}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}&\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta}\\
\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|[a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{x})a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y}),A]\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}&\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/2}\\
\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|[\mathcal{T}_{1}^{*}(x,y),A]\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}&\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1}\\
\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}&\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1+\beta/4}.\end{split}

(136)

Together with (
133
), these bounds can be used to control the first 4 terms on the r.h.s. of (
135
). In Lemma
23
, we will also show that

\Big|\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\big[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),[a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y}),A^{*}]\big]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}\,.

(137)

By (
135
), this implies that

\Big|\int dxdyV_{N}(x-y)\langle\xi_{s}a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.

(138)

Proceeding very similarly as in the proof of (
138
), we can also show

\Big|\int dxdyV_{N}(x-y)\langle\xi a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})\xi\rangle\Big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.

The only term for which the details of the analysis needs some additional adjustments is

\begin{split}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\;\langle\Theta\xi_{s},[a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y}),A^{*}]\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle,\end{split}

which is of order
N^{4\kappa-1+\epsilon-\beta/4}
.

From (
131
), we obtain
\langle\xi,\tilde{\mathcal{V}}_{N}^{(0)}\xi\rangle\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}
, as claimed.
∎

In the next lemma, we show the bounds (
133
).

Lemma 21
.

As in the proof of Lemma
20
, we let
\xi_{s}=e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
, for all
s\in[0;1]
,
\theta=\gamma-1
,
V_{N}(x)=N^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)
. For every
\epsilon>0
, there is
C>0
such that

\begin{split}T_{0}&:=\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/4+\epsilon}\\
T_{1}&:=\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|a_{x}a(\check{\theta}_{y})\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/2+\epsilon}\end{split}

(139)

for all
s\in[0;1]
, if
\alpha
and then
n
are chosen sufficiently large in the definition (
89
) of the cutoff
\Theta
.

Proof.

We write

\begin{split}T_{0}&=\int dxdydrdr^{\prime}dtdt^{\prime}\,\check{\theta}(r)\check{\theta}(r^{\prime})\check{\theta}(t)\check{\theta}(t^{\prime})|V_{N}(x-y)|\langle\Theta\xi_{s},a^{*}_{y-t}a^{*}_{x-r}a_{y-t^{\prime}}a_{x-r^{\prime}}\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle.\end{split}

(140)

Next, we localize
\check{\theta}=\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\check{\theta}_{u}
, where we recall that
\check{\theta}_{u}=\check{\theta}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}
and
B_{u}=\{x\in\Lambda,\inf_{v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lvert x-v\rvert=\lvert x-u\rvert\}
with
\Lambda_{B}=\Lambda\cap\ell_{B}\mathbb{Z}^{3}
. With Cauchy-Schwarz, we obtain

\begin{split}|T_{0}|&\leq\sum_{u,u^{\prime},v,v^{\prime}}\Big(\int dxdydrdr^{\prime}dtdt^{\prime}\;|V_{N}(x-y)|\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(r)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v}}(t)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times\check{\theta}_{u^{\prime}}(r^{\prime})^{2}\check{\theta}_{v^{\prime}}(t^{\prime})^{2}\;\|a_{y-t}a_{x-r}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 2.84544pt\times\Big(\int dxdydrdr^{\prime}dtdt^{\prime}\;|V_{N}(x-y)|\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u^{\prime}}}(r^{\prime})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v^{\prime}}}(t^{\prime})\check{\theta}_{u}(r)^{2}\check{\theta}_{v}(t)^{2}\|a_{y-{t^{\prime}}}a_{x-{r^{\prime}}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\leq CN^{\epsilon}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\ell_{B}^{3}\Big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{\theta}_{u}\rVert_{2}\Big)^{4}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}.\end{split}

With Lemma
6
, Lemma
15
and since
\|V_{N}\|_{1}\leq CN^{-1+\kappa}
, we conclude that

|T_{0}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/4+\epsilon}.

The second bound in (
139
) can be shown similarly. In this case, we can remove two factors of
\check{\theta}
and the integrals over
r,r^{\prime}
; as a result, we obtain

|T_{1}|\leq CN^{\epsilon}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\Big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{\theta}_{u}\rVert_{2}\Big)^{2}\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{N}\xi_{s}\rangle\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/2+\epsilon}.

∎

Next, we show the estimates (
136
).

Lemma 22
.

As in the proof of Lemma
20
, we let
\xi_{s}=e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
, for all
s\in[0;1]
,
\theta=1-\gamma
,
V_{N}(x)=N^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)
. For any
\epsilon>0
, we have

\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{3}^{*}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta}

(141)

\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|[a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{x})a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y}),A]\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/2}

(142)

\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|[\mathcal{T}_{1}^{*}(x,y),A]\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1}

(143)

\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{\epsilon}N^{4\kappa-1+\beta/4}

(144)

if
\alpha
and then
n
are chosen large enough.

Proof.

From (
134
), we find

\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)\mathcal{T}_{3}^{*}(x,y)=\mathcal{T}_{3,2}+\mathcal{T}_{3,4}+\mathcal{T}_{3,6}

with

\begin{split}\mathcal{T}_{3,2}(x,y)&=\sum\int dx_{1}dx_{2}\,\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z_{1})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-w_{1})a_{z_{2}}^{*}a_{w_{2}}\\
\mathcal{T}_{3,4}(x,y)&=\sum\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z_{1})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{3}-x_{2},x_{3}-w_{1})a_{x_{3}}^{*}a_{z_{2}}^{*}a_{w_{2}}a_{x_{1}}\\
\mathcal{T}_{3,6}(x,y)&=\sum\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z_{1})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-w_{1})\\
&\hskip 227.62204pt\times a_{z_{2}}^{*}a_{x_{3}}^{*}a_{x_{4}}^{*}a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}a_{w_{2}}\end{split}

where the sums run over the four possible choices of
(z_{1},z_{2}),(w_{1},w_{2})\in\{(x,y),(y,x)\}
(i.e. we either have
z_{1}=x,z_{2}=y
or
z_{1}=y,z_{2}=x
and similarly for
(w_{1},w_{2})
). Let us denote by
\mathcal{T}_{3,2}^{(0)}(x,y)
the contribution to
\mathcal{T}_{3,2}(x,y)
associated with
z_{1}=w_{1}=x
,
z_{2}=w_{2}=y
. With Cauchy-Schwarz, we can estimate

\begin{split}\Big|\int dxdy&|V_{N}(x-y)|\langle\xi,\mathcal{T}_{3,2}^{(0)}(x,y)\xi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&\Big(\int dxdydx_{1}dx_{2}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x)^{2}\|a_{y}\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydx_{1}dx_{2}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x)^{2}\|a_{y}\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\|V_{N}\|_{1}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi\|^{2}\|A^{(6)}\|_{2}\|A^{(3)}\|_{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta}\end{split}

where we used
\|V_{N}\|_{1}\leq CN^{-1+\kappa}
, the bounds in Lemma
9
(in particular,
\|A^{(3)}\|_{2},A^{(6)}\|_{2}\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2}
) and the estimate
\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi\|^{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}
from Lemma
15
. The other contributions arising from
\mathcal{T}_{3,2}
can be bounded similarly.

Next, let us denote by
\mathcal{T}_{3,4}^{(0)}(x,y)
the contribution to
\mathcal{T}_{3,4}(x,y)
associated with
z_{1}=w_{1}=x
and
z_{2}=w_{2}=y
. We can estimate

\begin{split}\Big|\int dxdy&|V_{N}(x-y)|\langle\Theta\xi,\mathcal{T}_{3,4}^{(0)}(x,y)\Theta\xi\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&\sum_{u,u^{\prime},v,v^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{B}}\int dxdydx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}|V_{N}(x-y)|\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\lvert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x)\rvert\lvert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}}(x_{3}-x_{2},x_{3}-x)\rvert\|a_{x_{1}}a_{y}\Theta\xi\|\|a_{x_{3}}a_{y}\Theta\xi\|\\
\leq\;&\sum_{u,u^{\prime},v,v^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{B}}\Big(\int dxdydx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\mathbbm{1}(|x_{3}-x_{2}-u^{\prime}|\leq\ell_{B})\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times|\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x)|^{2}\|a_{x_{3}}a_{y}\Theta\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 54.06006pt\times\Big(\int dxdydx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-x_{2}-u|\leq\ell_{B})\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times|\check{A}^{(6)}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}}(x_{3}-x_{2},x_{3}-x)|^{2}\|a_{x_{1}}a_{y}\Theta\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&CN^{\epsilon}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi\|^{2}\Big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}\rVert_{2}\Big)^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta+\epsilon}.\end{split}

The other contributions arising from
\mathcal{T}_{3,4}
can be handled similarly. Also the contributions from
\mathcal{T}_{3,6}
can be estimated analogously.

Next, we consider the bound (
142
). Similarly to (
134
), we have

[a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{x})a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y}),A]=-\int drdt\;\check{\theta}(r)\check{\theta}(t)\big(\mathcal{T}_{1}(x-r,y-t)+\mathcal{T}_{3}(x-r,y-t)\big).

(145)

Therefore, we find

\begin{split}\int dxdy\,|V_{N}&(x-y)|\|\big[a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{x})a^{*}(\check{\theta}_{y}),A\big]\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\\
&\leq\sum_{i=1,3}\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\Big\|\int drdt\,\check{\theta}(r)\check{\theta}(t)\mathcal{T}_{i}(x-r,y-t)\Theta\xi_{s}\Big\|^{2}.\end{split}

(146)

To bound the term with
i=3
, we observe that

\begin{split}\Big\|&\int drdt\,\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\mathcal{T}_{3}(r,t)\Theta\xi_{s}\Big\|^{2}\\
=\;&\int drdtdr^{\prime}dt^{\prime}\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\check{\theta}(x-r^{\prime})\check{\theta}(y-t^{\prime})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\{(r,t),(t,r)\},\\
(w_{1},w_{2})\in\{(r^{\prime},t^{\prime}),(t^{\prime},r^{\prime})\}\end{subarray}}\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,\\
&\times\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z_{1})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-w_{1})\langle\Theta\xi_{s},a^{*}_{z_{2}}a^{*}_{x_{3}}a^{*}_{x_{4}}a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}a_{w_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\\
&+\int drdtdr^{\prime}\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(z_{1},z_{2})\in\{(r,t),(t,r)\}\end{subarray}}\big(\check{\theta}(x-r^{\prime})\check{\theta}(y-z_{2})+\check{\theta}(x-z_{2})\check{\theta}(y-r^{\prime})\big)\\
&\times\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z_{1})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-r^{\prime})\langle\Theta\xi_{s},a^{*}_{x_{3}}a^{*}_{x_{4}}a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\\
=\;&\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,6}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle+\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,4}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle.\end{split}

Let us denote by
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,6}^{(0)}(x,y)
the contribution to
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,6}(x,y)
associated with the choice
z_{1}=r,w_{1}=r^{\prime},z_{2}=t,w_{2}=t^{\prime}
. Localizing the kernels
\check{\theta}
and
\check{A}^{(3)}
and applying Cauchy-Schwarz, we can estimate

\begin{split}\Big|\int dxdy\,&|V_{N}(x-y)|\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\tilde{\mathcal{T}}^{(0)}_{3,6}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&\sum_{u,u^{\prime},v,v^{\prime},w,w^{\prime},m,m^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{B}}\Big(\int drdtdr^{\prime}dt^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}dxdy\,\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\\
&\hskip 36.98866pt\times\check{\theta}_{u}(x-r)^{2}\check{\theta}_{u^{\prime}}(y-t)^{2}\,\check{A}^{(3)}_{m^{\prime},w^{\prime}}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-r^{\prime})^{2}\\
&\hskip 36.98866pt\times\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v}}(x-r^{\prime})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v^{\prime}}}(y-t^{\prime})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{m}}(x_{1}-x_{2})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{w}}(x_{1}-r)\,\|a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}a_{t^{\prime}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\Big(\int drdtdr^{\prime}dt^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}dxdy\;|V_{N}(x-y)|\\
&\hskip 36.98866pt\times\check{\theta}_{v}(x-r^{\prime})^{2}\check{\theta}_{v^{\prime}}(y-t^{\prime})^{2}\check{A}^{(3)}_{m,w}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-r)^{2}\\
&\hskip 36.98866pt\times\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(x-r)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u^{\prime}}}(y-t)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{m^{\prime}}}(x_{3}-x_{4})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{w^{\prime}}}(x_{3}-r^{\prime})\|a_{x_{4}}a_{x_{3}}a_{t}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&CN^{\epsilon}\ell_{B}^{6}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}\rVert_{2}\Big)^{2}\Big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{\theta}_{u}\rVert_{2}\Big)^{4}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/2+\epsilon}.\end{split}

where we used
\|V_{N}\|_{1}\leq CN^{-1+\kappa}
,
\sum_{u}\|\check{\theta}_{u}\|_{2}\leq N^{3\kappa/4+\epsilon}
from Lemma
6
,
\sum_{u,v}\|A^{(3)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2}
from Lemma
9
and the estimate
\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi\|^{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}
from Lemma
15
. The other contributions arising from
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,6}(x,y)
can be controlled analogously.

As for the term
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,4}(x,y)
, we consider the contribution
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}^{(0)}_{3,4}(x,y)
, associated with
z_{1}=r
,
z_{2}=t
, proportional to
\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(x-r^{\prime})\check{\theta}(y-t)^{2}
. Localizing all kernels
\check{\theta},\check{A}^{(3)}
, we find

\begin{split}\Big|\int&dxdy|V_{N}(x-y)|\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\tilde{\mathcal{T}}^{(0)}_{3,4}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&\sum_{u,u^{\prime},v,v^{\prime},w,w^{\prime},m\in\Lambda_{B}}\Big(\int dxdydx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}drdtdr^{\prime}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\check{\theta}_{m}(y-t)^{2}\check{\theta}_{w}(x-r)^{2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-r^{\prime})^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{w^{\prime}}}(x-r^{\prime})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v^{\prime}}}(x_{1}-r)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u^{\prime}}}(x_{1}-x_{2})\|a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 42.67912pt\times\Big(\int dxdydx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}drdtdr^{\prime}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\check{\theta}_{m}(y-t)^{2}\check{\theta}_{w^{\prime}}(x-r^{\prime})^{2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-r)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{w}}(x-r)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v}}(x_{3}-r^{\prime})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(x_{3}-x_{4})\|a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\Theta\xi\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\leq CN^{\epsilon}\ell_{B}^{6}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi\|^{2}\Big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}\rVert_{2}\Big)^{2}\Big(\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\lVert\check{\theta}_{u}\rVert_{2}\Big)^{4}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/2+\epsilon}.\end{split}

The other contributions arising from
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,4}(x,y)
can be handled similarly. As for the term with
i=1
on the r.h.s of (
146
), we compute

\begin{split}\Big\|\int drdt\;&\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\mathcal{T}_{1}(r,t)\Theta\xi_{s}\Big\|^{2}=\\
=&\int drdtdr^{\prime}dt^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}\,\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\check{\theta}(x-r^{\prime})\check{\theta}(y-t^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-t,x_{1}-r)\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{2}-t^{\prime},x_{2}-r^{\prime})\langle\xi,a^{*}_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}\xi\rangle.\end{split}

Hence, proceeding as we did above to control the terms
\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,6}^{(0)},\tilde{\mathcal{T}}_{3,4}^{(0)}
, we find

\begin{split}\int&dxdy|V_{N}(x-y)|\Big\|\int drdt\;\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\mathcal{T}_{1}(r,t)\Theta\xi_{s}\Big\|^{2}\\
\leq\;&C\ell_{B}^{6}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\|A^{(6)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\Big)^{2}\Big(\sum_{w\in\Lambda_{B}}\|\check{\theta}_{w}\|_{2}\Big)^{4}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/2+\epsilon}.\end{split}

Next, we show (
143
). From (
134
), we find

[\mathcal{T}^{*}_{1}(x,y),A]=\int dzdx_{1}dx_{2}\,\check{A}^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{2}-z)a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}.

(147)

Hence

\begin{split}\|[\mathcal{T}_{1}^{*}&(x,y),A]\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\\
=&\;\int dzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,\check{A}^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{2}-z)\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y,z^{\prime}-x)\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{4}-z^{\prime})\langle\Theta\xi,a^{*}_{x_{3}}a^{*}_{x_{4}}a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi\rangle.\end{split}

(148)

Therefore, setting
D^{(6)}(x)=\sup_{s}|A^{(6)}(x,s)|
and
D_{u}^{(6)}(x)=D^{(6)}(x)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(x)
for
u\in\Lambda_{B}
, we obtain

\begin{split}\int&dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|[\mathcal{T}_{1}^{*}(x,y),A]\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\\
\leq\;&\sum_{w,u,v,w^{\prime},u^{\prime},v^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{B}}\Big(\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|D_{w}^{(6)}(z-y)^{2}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}_{u^{\prime},v^{\prime}}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{4}-z^{\prime})^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{w^{\prime}}}(z^{\prime}-y)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(x_{1}-x_{2})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v}}(x_{2}-z)\|a_{x_{1}}a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|D_{w^{\prime}}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y)^{2}\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{2}-z)^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{w}}(z-y)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u^{\prime}}}(x_{3}-x_{4})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v^{\prime}}}(x_{4}-z^{\prime})\|a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&CN^{\epsilon}\ell_{B}^{6}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\,\Big(\sum_{u,v}\|A_{u,v}^{(3)}\|_{2}\Big)^{2}\Big(\sum_{w}\|D^{(6)}_{w}\|_{2}\Big)^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1+\epsilon}\end{split}

where we used the bounds in Lemma
9
and Lemma
15
to estimate
\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa-1}
.

Finally, we consider (
144
). From (
134
), setting again
D^{(6)}(x)=\sup_{s}|A^{(6)}(s,x)|
, we find

\begin{split}\int dxdy\,&|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\\
&=\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|A^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)A^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y,z^{\prime}-x)\langle a_{z}\Theta\xi_{s},a_{z^{\prime}}\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\\
&\leq\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|D^{(6)}(z-x)D^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-x)\|a_{z}\Theta\xi_{s}\|\|a_{z^{\prime}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|.\end{split}

Localizing
D^{(6)}
and applying Cauchy-Schwarz, we arrive at

\begin{split}\int dx&dy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\\
\leq\;&\sum_{u,u^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{B}}\Big(\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,|V_{N}(x-y)||D_{u}^{(6)}(z-x)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u^{\prime}}}(z^{\prime}-x)\|a_{z^{\prime}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\Big(\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,|V_{N}(x-y)||D_{u^{\prime}}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-x)|^{2}\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(z-x)\|a_{z}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\ell_{B}^{3}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\Big(\sum_{u}\|D^{(6)}_{u}\|_{2}\Big)^{2}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1+\beta/4+\epsilon}.\end{split}

∎

Finally, we prove (
137
).

Lemma 23
.

As in the proof of Lemma
20
, we let
\xi_{s}=e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
, for all
s\in[0;1]
,
\theta=\gamma-1
,
V_{N}(x)=N^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)
. Then

\Big|\int dxdy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\big[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),[a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y}),A^{*}]\big]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}\,.

Proof.

From (
145
), we find

\begin{split}\int dx&dy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\big[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),[a(\check{\theta}_{x})a(\check{\theta}_{y}),A^{*}]\big]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\\
&=\sum_{j=1,3}\int dxdydrdt\,\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\big[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),\mathcal{T}_{j}^{*}(r,t)\big]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle.\end{split}

(149)

Let us first focus on
j=3
. With (
134
), we compute

\begin{split}\big[\mathcal{T}_{1}&(x,y),\mathcal{T}_{3}^{*}(r,t)\big]\\
&=\int dx_{1}dx_{2}\,\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}(z_{1},w_{1})\in\{(x_{1},x_{2}),(x_{2},x_{1})\}\\
(z_{2},w_{2})\in\{(r,t),(t,r)\}\end{subarray}}A^{(6)}(z_{1}-y,z_{1}-x)A^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z_{2})a_{w_{1}}^{*}a_{w_{2}}.\end{split}

The contribution of the term
[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),\mathcal{T}^{*}_{3}(r,t)]^{(0)}
, associated with
z_{1}=x_{1},w_{1}=x_{2},z_{2}=r,w_{2}=t
, to (
149
) can be bounded, localizing the kernels
\check{\theta}
and
A^{(3)}
and introducing
D^{(6)}(x)=\sup_{s}A^{(6)}(s,x)
, by

\begin{split}\Big|\int dx&dy\,V_{N}(x-y)\,\langle\Theta\xi_{s},[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),\mathcal{T}^{*}_{3}(r,t)]^{(0)}\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\\
=\;&\Big|\int dxdydrdtdx_{1}dx_{2}V_{N}(x-y)\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times A^{(6)}(x_{1}-y,x_{1}-x)A^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-r)\langle\Theta\xi_{s},a_{x_{2}}^{*}a_{t}\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\\
\leq\;&\sum_{u,v,w,w^{\prime}\in\Lambda_{B}}\Big(\int dxdydrdtdx_{1}dx_{2}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\check{\theta}_{w}(x-r)^{2}\check{\theta}_{w^{\prime}}(y-t)^{2}\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times A^{(3)}_{u,v}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-r)^{2}\|a_{t}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydrdtdx_{1}dx_{2}\,|V_{N}(x-y)|D^{(6)}(x_{1}-x)^{2}\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\mathbbm{1}_{B_{w^{\prime}}}(y-t)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(x_{1}-x_{2})\mathbbm{1}_{B_{v}}(x_{1}-r)\|a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&C\ell_{B}^{9/2}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\|D^{(6)}\|_{2}\Big(\sum_{u,v}\|A^{(3)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\Big)\Big(\sum_{w}\|\check{\theta}_{w}\|_{2}\Big)^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/8+\epsilon}.\end{split}

The other contributions arising from the commutator
[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),\mathcal{T}^{*}_{3}(r,t)]
can be bounded similarly. As for the term on the r.h.s. of (
149
) with
j=1
, we compute

[\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y),\mathcal{T}^{*}_{1}(r,t)]=\int dz\,\check{A}^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)\check{A}^{(6)}(z-t,z-r).

The contribution of this term to (
149
) can be estimated switching to Fourier space. We find

\begin{split}\Big|\int dxdy&drdtdzV_{N}(x-y)\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\check{A}^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)\check{A}^{(6)}(z-t,z-r)\Big|\\
