&=\Big|\sum_{p,q,k\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}_{N}(k)\theta_{q}\theta_{p}A^{(6)}_{q-k,p+k}A^{(6)}_{q,p}\Big|\leq CN^{-1+\kappa}\sum_{k,p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}|\theta_{q}||\theta_{p}||A^{(6)}_{q-k,p+k}||A^{(6)}_{p,q}|.\end{split}

With the bounds in Lemma
9
,
we conclude that

\Big|\int dxdydrdtdzV_{N}(x-y)\check{\theta}(x-r)\check{\theta}(y-t)\check{A}^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)\check{A}^{(6)}(z-t,z-r)\Big|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.

∎

3.3
Bounds for relevant cubic expectations

In this subsection, we conclude the estimate for the energy of the trial state
\psi_{N}=W_{N_{0}}e^{B^{*}-B}\xi
, proving bounds for the expectations
\langle\xi,(\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*}+\mathcal{C}_{N})\xi\rangle
,
\langle\xi,\mathcal{V}_{N}\xi\rangle
and
\langle\xi,\mathcal{K}\xi\rangle
, with
\xi=e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
.

Lemma 24
.

In position space, we write the operator
\mathcal{C}_{N}
from (
106
) as

\mathcal{C}_{N}=N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzN^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}(x-y))\check{\sigma}(x-z)a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a^{*}_{z}.

(150)

Recalling the definition (
75
) of the kernel
\check{A}^{(6)}
, we find

\begin{split}\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,&(\mathcal{C}_{N}+\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*})e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&=2N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdyN^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)\check{\sigma}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(x,y)+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(151)

Proof.

Expanding
e^{-A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }
, recalling the notation
\xi_{s}=e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
,
V_{N}(x)=N^{2-2-\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)
and the definition (
134
) of the operators
\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y),\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)
, we find

\begin{split}&\langle\xi,\mathcal{C}_{N}\xi\rangle\\
&=N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\int dxdydz\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\langle\xi_{s},[A,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}]e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}\xi\rangle+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\\
&=N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\int dxdydz\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}\xi\rangle\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt+N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\int dxdydz\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\langle\Theta\xi_{s},\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}\xi\rangle+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\\
&=:S_{1}+S_{3}+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\end{split}

(152)

where the error term
\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}
includes the contribution of all terms proportional to
1-\Theta
(to handle these terms and show that
\pm\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\leq CN^{-\nu}
for arbitrary
\nu>0
, we can proceed as in (
132
), choosing
\alpha>0
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
large enough). We can bound

\begin{split}|S_{3}|\leq\;&CN^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\Big(\int dxdy|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{3}^{*}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\Big(\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(y)|\Big\|\int dz\,\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{z}^{*}\xi\Big\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}.\end{split}

(153)

From Lemma
22
, we find

\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\|\mathcal{T}_{3}^{*}(x,y)\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta+\epsilon}

for any
\epsilon>0
. On the other hand, we have

\int dx\,\Big\|\int dz\,\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{z}^{*}\xi_{s}\Big\|^{2}=\|\check{\sigma}\|_{2}^{2}+\|\tilde{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}

(154)

for all
s\in[0;1]
, by Lemma
5
and Lemma
19
. With
\|V_{N}\|_{1}\leq CN^{-1+\kappa}
, we conclude that

|S_{3}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}N^{-\beta/4+\epsilon}

for any
\epsilon>0
.

As for the term
S_{1}
, we expand

\begin{split}S_{1}=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y,z^{\prime}-x)\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\langle\xi,e^{(1-s)A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z^{\prime}}e^{-(1-s)A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}\xi\rangle\\
=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y,z^{\prime}-x)\langle\xi,a_{z^{\prime}}a_{z}^{*}\xi\rangle\\
&+N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y,z^{\prime}-x)\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\langle\xi,e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]e^{-tA^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}\xi\rangle\end{split}

and thus

\begin{split}S_{1}=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydz\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(z-y,z-x)\\
&+N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y,z^{\prime}-x)\langle\xi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}\xi\rangle\\
&+N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(z^{\prime}-y,z^{\prime}-x)\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\langle\xi_{1-t},[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]e^{-tA^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\xi_{1-t}\rangle.\end{split}

Expanding
e^{-tA^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }
and using the symmetry (
76
), we conclude that

\begin{split}S_{1}=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdy\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)\\
&+N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\langle\xi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}\xi\rangle\\
&+N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 256.0748pt\times\langle\xi_{1-t},[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]a_{z}^{*}\xi_{1-t}\rangle\\
&+N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int_{0}^{t}d\tau\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\langle\xi_{1-t},[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]e^{-\tau A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }[A,a_{z}^{*}]\xi_{1-t+\tau}\rangle\\
=:\;&S_{1,1}+S_{1,2}+S_{1,3}+S_{1,4}.\end{split}

(155)

The term
S_{1,1}
produces the main term on the r.h.s. of (
151
). To control
S_{1,2},S_{1,3},S_{1,4}
, we need bounds for

\begin{split}\int dx\Big\|\int dz^{\prime}\,\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})a_{z^{\prime}}\xi_{s}\Big\|^{2}&=\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\\
\int dx\Big\|\int dz\,\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z)\big[a_{z}^{*},A\big]\xi_{s}\Big\|^{2}&=\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}\xi_{s}\|^{2}\end{split}

where we set
A_{y}(x)=\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)
. To obtain such bounds, we use Lemma
19
(and the subsequent remark). We have

\begin{split}\langle\Omega,&[A,[\mathcal{N}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle=\int dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dzdz^{\prime}\,\check{A}^{(6)}(y,z^{\prime}-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,z^{\prime}-x_{3})\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-x_{3})\check{A}^{(6)}(z-x_{1},z-x_{2}).\end{split}

Switching to Fourier, we find

\langle\Omega,[A,[\mathcal{N}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle=\sum_{p,q,r,s\in\Lambda^{*}}A^{(6)}_{r,q}A^{(6)}_{s,q}A^{(3)}_{p,q}A^{(6)}_{p,q}e^{i(r+s)\cdot y}.

With Lemma
5
and Lemma
9
, we conclude that

\begin{split}|\langle\Omega,[A,[\mathcal{N}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}},A^{*}]]\Omega\rangle|&\leq\sum_{p,q,r,s}|A^{(6)}_{r,q}||A^{(6)}_{s,q}||A^{(3)}_{p,q}||A^{(6)}_{p,q}|\\
&\leq N\sum_{j=3,6}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}(1+\sigma^{2}_{q})|A_{p,q}^{(j)}|^{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa}\end{split}

uniformly in
y
.

On the other hand, setting
D^{(6)}(x)=\sup_{y}\lvert\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)\rvert
and
D_{u}^{(6)}(x)=D^{(6)}(x)\mathbbm{1}_{B_{u}}(x)
for every
u\in\Lambda_{B}
, we observe that, with Lemma
9
,

\sup_{y}\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|(A_{y})_{u}\|_{2}\leq\sum_{u\in\Lambda_{B}}\|D^{(6)}_{u}\|_{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa/4+1/2+\epsilon}

for any
\epsilon>0
. Hence, Lemma
19
(and the remark after it) implies that

\begin{split}\sup_{y}\|\mathcal{N}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}&\leq CN^{3\kappa}(1+N^{-\beta/8+\epsilon})\\
\sup_{y}\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|^{2}&\leq CN^{3\kappa-\beta/8+\epsilon}\end{split}

for every
\epsilon>0
. Choosing
\epsilon>0
small enough, we conclude that

\sup_{y}\|\mathcal{N}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|,\;\sup_{y}\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}

(156)

for all
s\in[0;1]
. With (
156
) and with the bounds

\begin{split}\int dx\Big\|\int dz\,\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{z}\xi_{s}\|^{2}=\|\mathcal{N}_{\check{\sigma}\star\check{\sigma}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|=\|\tilde{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2}\\
\int dx\Big\|\int dz\,\check{\sigma}(x-z)[a^{*}_{z},A]\xi_{s}\|^{2}=\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}_{\check{\sigma}\star\check{\sigma}}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|\leq CN^{(3\kappa-1)/2}\end{split}

which also follow from Lemma
19
and the subsequent remark, we can now estimate the r.h.s. of (
155
). We have

\begin{split}|S_{1,2}|\leq\;&CN^{1/2}\int dxdy|V_{N}(y)|\,\Big\|\int dz\,\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{z}\xi\Big\|\Big\|\int dz^{\prime}\,\check{A}^{(6)}_{y}(x-z^{\prime})a_{z^{\prime}}\xi\Big\|\\
\leq\;&CN^{1/2}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\,\|\tilde{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}\xi\|\sup_{y}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}\xi\|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}\end{split}

(157)

and, similarly,

\begin{split}|S_{1,4}|\leq\;&CN^{1/2}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int_{0}^{t}d\tau\,\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}_{\check{\sigma}\star\check{\sigma}}\xi_{1-t+\tau}\|\,\sup_{y}\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}\xi_{1-t}\|\\
\leq\;&CN^{4\kappa-1}.\end{split}

(158)

To estimate
S_{1,3}
, we expand it once again, writing

\begin{split}S_{1,3}=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int_{0}^{1-t}d\tau\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 99.58464pt\times\big(\langle\xi_{\tau},[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}][A,a_{z}^{*}]\xi_{\tau}\rangle+\langle\xi_{\tau},[A,[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]]a_{z}^{*}\xi_{\tau}\rangle\big)\\
=:\;&S_{1,3,1}+S_{1.3.2}\end{split}

(159)

We find

|S_{1,3,1}|\leq CN^{1/2}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\,\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}_{\check{\sigma}\star\check{\sigma}}\xi_{\tau}\|\,\sup_{y}\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}_{A_{y}\star A_{y}}\xi_{\tau}\|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.

As for
S_{1,3,2}
, we write

S_{1,3,2}=T_{0}+T_{2}+T_{4}+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}

(160)

with

\begin{split}T_{0}=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int_{0}^{1-t}d\tau\,\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 156.49014pt\times\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z)\\
T_{2}=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int_{0}^{1-t}d\tau\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{3}-x_{2},x_{3}-z)\langle\xi_{\tau},a_{x_{1}}^{*}a_{x_{3}}\xi_{\tau}\rangle\\
&+N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int_{0}^{1-t}d\tau\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{3}-x_{1},x_{3}-x_{2})\langle\xi_{\tau},a_{z}^{*}a_{x_{3}}\xi_{\tau}\rangle\\
T_{4}=\;&N_{0}^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{1-s}dt\int_{0}^{1-t}d\tau\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z)\\
&\times\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-x_{1})\langle\Theta\xi_{\tau},a_{z}^{*}a_{x_{2}}^{*}a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\Theta\xi_{\tau}\rangle\end{split}

and where
\pm\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\leq CN^{-\nu}
for arbitrary
\nu>0
, if
\alpha>0
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
are large enough. The identity (
160
) follows similarly as (
128
), because the operator

N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x-z),\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]a_{z}^{*}

has exactly the form of
[\mathcal{N}_{g},A^{*}]
in (
127
), if we choose

g(z)=N_{0}^{1/2}\int dxdy\,V_{N}(y)\check{\sigma}(x)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z).

In contrast to (
128
), however, the contribution
T_{4,2}
does not appear in (
160
) (it corresponds to the term
S_{1,3,1}
). To bound
T_{0}
, we switch to Fourier space. We find

T_{0}=\frac{N_{0}^{1/2}}{3}\sum_{r,k,p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}_{N}(r)\sigma_{k}\,A^{(6)}_{r,k}A^{(3)}_{p,k}A^{(6)}_{p,k}.

(161)

With Lemma
9
(and Lemma
5
, for the bound
\|\sigma\|_{4}^{4}\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}
), we find

|T_{0}|\leq CN\|\hat{V}_{N}\|_{\infty}\sum_{j=3,6}\sum_{p,k\in\Lambda^{*}}(1+\sigma_{k}^{2})|A_{p,k}^{(j)}|^{2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.

Also
T_{2}
is conveniently expressed in Fourier space as

\begin{split}T_{2}=\;&\frac{N_{0}^{1/2}}{2}\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\,(1-\tau^{2})\\
&\hskip 8.5359pt\times\sum_{r,k,p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}_{N}(r)\,\sigma_{k}\,A_{r,k}^{(6)}\,A_{p,k}^{(3)}A_{p,k}^{(6)}\,\big\langle\xi_{\tau},\big(a_{k}^{*}a_{k}+a_{p}^{*}a_{p}+a_{p+k}^{*}a_{p+k}\big)\xi_{\tau}\big\rangle.\end{split}

(162)

With
\|\hat{V}_{N}\|_{\infty}\leq CN^{-1+\kappa}
,
\|\sigma\|_{\infty}\leq CN^{\beta/8}
, Lemma
9
(and the identity
A^{(6)}_{p,k}=A^{(6)}_{p+k,-k}
to handle the contribution proportional to
a_{p+k}^{*}a_{p+k}
), we obtain

|T_{2}|\leq CN^{\kappa-\beta/4}\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\,\big\langle\xi_{\tau},(\mathcal{N}+\tilde{\mathcal{N}})\xi_{\tau}\rangle.

With Lemma
15
and Lemma
19
, we conclude that
|T_{2}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/4}
. Finally, we consider
T_{4}
. Setting
D^{(6)}(x)=\sup_{y}|\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)|
, we can bound

\begin{split}|T_{4}|\leq\;&CN^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\int dxdydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|V_{N}(y)||D^{(6)}(x-z^{\prime})||\check{A}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})|\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times|\check{A}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-x_{1})|\|a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\Theta\xi_{\tau}\|\Big\|\int dz\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{z}a_{x_{2}}\Theta\xi_{\tau}\Big\|.\end{split}

Next, we localize the kernels
D^{(6)},\check{A}^{(6)}
and we apply Cauchy-Schwarz. We find

\begin{split}|T_{4}|\leq\;&CN^{1/2}\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\sum_{u_{1},u_{2},v_{1},v_{2},w\in\Lambda_{B}}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|V_{N}(y)||D_{w}^{(6)}(x-z^{\prime})|^{2}|A_{u_{1},v_{1}}^{(3)}(x_{3}-x_{4},x_{3}-x_{1})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 36.98866pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}-x_{2}-u_{2}|,|x_{1}-z^{\prime}-v_{2}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\Big\|\int dz\,\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{x_{2}}a_{z}\Theta\xi_{\tau}\Big\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|V_{N}(y)||A_{u_{2},v_{2}}^{(6)}(x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1}-z^{\prime})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 36.98866pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x-z^{\prime}-w|,|x_{3}-x_{4}-u_{1}|,|x_{3}-x_{1}-v_{1}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{x_{3}}a_{x_{4}}\Theta\xi_{\tau}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}.\end{split}

Applying Lemma
11
and appropriately shifting the integration variables, we obtain

\begin{split}|T_{4}|\leq\;&CN^{1/2+\epsilon}\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\sum_{u_{1},u_{2},v_{1},v_{2},w\in\Lambda_{B}}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|V_{N}(y)||D_{w}^{(6)}(z^{\prime})|^{2}|A_{u_{1},v_{1}}^{(3)}(x_{4},x_{3})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x_{1}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\Big\|\int dz\,\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{z}\xi_{\tau}\Big\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydz^{\prime}dx_{1}dx_{2}dx_{3}dx_{4}\,|V_{N}(y)||A_{u_{2},v_{2}}^{(6)}(x_{2},z^{\prime})|^{2}\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\mathbbm{1}(|x|,|x_{1}|\leq 10\ell_{B})\|a_{x_{3}}\xi_{\tau}\|^{2}\Big)^{1/2}\\
\leq\;&CN^{1/2+\epsilon}\|V_{N}\|_{1}\ell_{B}^{9/2}\big(\sum_{u}\|D^{(6)}_{u}\|_{2}\big)\big(\sum_{u,v}\|\check{A}^{(6)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\big)\big(\sum_{u,v}\|\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}\|_{2}\big)\\
&\hskip 199.16928pt\times\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\,\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{\tau}\|\|\tilde{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}\xi_{\tau}\|\\
\leq\;&CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/8+\epsilon}\end{split}

(163)

for any
\epsilon>0
. Choosing
\epsilon>0
small enough, we conclude that
|T_{4}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}
and therefore that
|S_{1,3}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}
. This concludes the proof of (
151
).
∎

In the next lemma, we estimate the expectation of the potential energy operator
\mathcal{V}_{N}
.

Lemma 25
.

From (
106
), recall the operator

\mathcal{V}_{N}=\frac{1}{2}\int dxdyN^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}(x-y))a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a_{x}a_{y}.

(164)

We have

\begin{split}\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,&\mathcal{V}_{N}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&=\frac{1}{2}\int dxdyN^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)\check{A}^{(6)}(x,y)^{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(165)

Proof.

With
\xi_{s}=e^{sA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
, we find, for any
t\in[0;1]
,

\begin{split}&\langle e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{V}_{N}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle=\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\big([\mathcal{V}_{N},A^{*}]+\text{h.c.}\ \big)\xi_{s}\rangle+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\end{split}

(166)

where, as usual,
\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}
collects all contributions proportional to
1-\Theta
. Note that we used
[\mathcal{V}_{N},\Theta]=0
as both are position space multiplication operators for each fixed number of particles. Using Lemma
11
and proceeding similarly as in (
132
), we can include this term in the error
O(N^{4\kappa-1})
, if we choose
\alpha>0
and
n\in\mathbb{N}
large enough. We write

\begin{split}[\mathcal{V}_{N},A^{*}]+\text{h.c.}\ =&\;\frac{1}{2}\int dxdydz\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{A}^{(6)}(x-y,x-z)a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}+\text{h.c.}\ \\
&+\int dxdydz_{1}dz_{2}\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{A}^{(3)}(z_{1}-z_{2},z_{1}-x)a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z_{1}}^{*}a_{z_{2}}^{*}a_{y}+\text{h.c.}\ \\
=&\;T_{3}+T_{5},\end{split}

(167)

where we used again the notation
V_{N}(x)=N^{2-2\kappa}V(N^{1-\kappa}x)
. To bound the expectation
\langle\xi_{s},T_{5}\xi_{s}\rangle
, we introduce the operator

\mathcal{W}_{N}=\frac{1}{2}\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(x-y)|\;a^{*}_{x}a^{*}_{y}a_{x}a_{y}.

and we localize the kernel
\check{A}^{(3)}
. Applying Cauchy-Schwarz and Lemma
11
, we find

\begin{split}&\lvert\langle\xi_{s},T_{5}\xi_{s}\rangle\rvert\\
&\leq\;C\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{\sigma}}\Big(\int dxdydz_{1}dz_{2}|V_{N}(x-y)||\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}(z_{1}-z_{2},z_{1}-x)|^{2}\|a_{y}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&\times\Big(\int dxdydz_{1}dz_{2}|V_{N}(x-y)|\mathbbm{1}(|z_{1}-z_{2}-u|,|z_{1}-x+v|\leq\ell_{B})\|a_{z_{1}}a_{z_{2}}a_{x}a_{y}\Theta\xi_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\\
\leq\;&CN^{\epsilon}\|V_{N}\|_{1}^{1/2}(\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{\sigma}}\|\check{A}^{(3)}_{u,v}\|)\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|\|\mathcal{W}_{N}^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}\end{split}

and thus

|\langle\xi_{s},T_{5}\xi_{s}\rangle|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}+CN^{-\beta+\epsilon}\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{W}_{N}\xi_{s}\rangle

(168)

for all
s\in[0;t]
. We consider now the expectation of the cubic term
T_{3}
in (
167
). We observe that this term has exactly the same form as the cubic operator
\mathcal{C}_{N}
considered in Lemma
24
, with
N_{0}^{1/2}\check{\sigma}(x-z)
replaced by
\check{A}^{(6)}(x-y,x-z)/2
. Thus, we can bound the expectation of
T_{3}
exactly as we estimated
\mathcal{C}_{N}
. Similarly as in (
152
), we write

\langle\xi_{s},T_{3}\xi_{s}\rangle=\text{Re }\big(\widetilde{S}_{1}+\widetilde{S}_{3}\big)+\mathcal{E}_{\Theta}

where

\begin{split}\widetilde{S}_{1}&=\int_{0}^{s}d\tau\int dxdydz\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{A}^{(6)}(x-y,x-z)\langle\xi_{\tau},\mathcal{T}_{1}(x,y)e^{-(1-\tau)A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}\xi_{s}\rangle\\
\widetilde{S}_{3}&=\int_{0}^{s}d\tau\int dxdydz\,V_{N}(x-y)\check{A}^{(6)}(x-y,x-z)\langle\xi_{\tau},\mathcal{T}_{3}(x,y)e^{-(1-\tau)A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }a_{z}^{*}\xi_{s}\rangle.\end{split}

Replacing the bound (
154
) by the estimate
\sup_{y}\|\check{A}^{(6)}(y,\cdot)\|_{2}\leq CN^{3\kappa/4+1/2}
and by (
156
), we obtain
|\widetilde{S}_{3}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/4+\epsilon}
. Proceeding as in the derivation of (
155
), we write

\begin{split}\widetilde{S}_{1}=\;&s\int dxdy\,V_{N}(y)\,|\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)|^{2}\\
&+s\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\langle\xi,a_{z}^{*}a_{z^{\prime}}\xi\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{s}d\tau_{1}\int_{0}^{1-\tau_{1}}d\tau_{2}\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 256.0748pt\times\langle\xi_{1-\tau_{2}},[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]a_{z}^{*}\xi_{1-\tau_{2}}\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{s}d\tau_{1}\int_{0}^{1-\tau_{1}}d\tau_{2}\int_{0}^{\tau_{2}}d\tau_{3}\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\\
&\hskip 170.71652pt\times\langle\xi_{1-\tau_{2}},[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]e^{-\tau A^{*}\Theta+\text{h.c.}\ }[A,a_{z}^{*}]\xi_{1-\tau_{2}+\tau_{3}}\rangle\\
=:\;&\widetilde{S}_{1,1}+\widetilde{S}_{1,2}+\widetilde{S}_{1,3}+\widetilde{S}_{1,4}.\end{split}

Analogously as in (
157
), (
158
), replacing the bounds

N^{1/2}\|\tilde{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}\xi\|\leq CN^{3\kappa/2},\quad N^{1/2}\|\mathcal{N}^{1/2}_{\check{\sigma}\star\check{\sigma}}\xi_{1-t-\tau}\|\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}

with the estimates (
156
), we obtain
|\widetilde{S}_{1,2}|,|\widetilde{S}_{1,4}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}
. Similarly to (
159
), we can decompose
\widetilde{S}_{1,3}=\widetilde{S}_{1,3,1}+\widetilde{S}_{1,3,2}
with

\begin{split}\widetilde{S}_{1,3,1}&=\int_{0}^{s}d\tau_{1}\int_{0}^{1-\tau_{1}}d\tau_{2}\int_{0}^{1-\tau_{2}}d\tau_{3}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\langle\xi_{\tau_{3}},[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}][A,a_{z}^{*}]\xi_{\tau_{3}}\rangle\\
\widetilde{S}_{1,3,2}&=\int_{0}^{s}d\tau_{1}\int_{0}^{1-\tau_{1}}d\tau_{2}\int_{0}^{1-\tau_{2}}d\tau_{3}\\
&\hskip 14.22636pt\times\int dxdydzdz^{\prime}\,V_{N}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z)\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z^{\prime})\langle\xi_{\tau_{3}},[A,[A^{*},a_{z^{\prime}}]]a_{z}^{*}\xi_{\tau_{3}}\rangle.\end{split}

Replacing again
N^{1/2}\|\ddot{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}_{\check{\sigma}\star\check{\sigma}}\xi_{\tau}\|\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}
with (
156
), we can show
|\widetilde{S}_{1,3,1}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}
. As for the term
\widetilde{S}_{1,3,2}
, we can write it as
\widetilde{S}_{1,3,2}=\widetilde{T}_{0}+\widetilde{T}_{2}+\widetilde{T}_{4}
, where
\widetilde{T}_{0},\widetilde{T}_{2},\widetilde{T}_{4}
are defined like the contributions
T_{0},T_{2},T_{4}
in (
160
), but with
N_{0}^{1/2}\check{\sigma}(x-z)
replaced by
\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x-z)
(and with adjusted time integrals). The bound
|\widetilde{T}_{4}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/8+\epsilon}
can be shown exactly as in (
163
) (replacing, in the last step, the estimate
N^{1/2}\|\tilde{\mathcal{N}}^{1/2}\xi_{\tau}\|\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}
by (
156
)). Similarly to (
161
), (
162
), we write

\begin{split}\widetilde{T}_{0}=\;&\frac{s}{2}(1-s^{2}/3)\sum_{r,q,k,p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}_{N}(r)A_{q,k}^{(6)}A^{(6)}_{q+r,k}A^{(3)}_{p,k}A^{(6)}_{p,k}\\
\widetilde{T}_{2}=\;&\int_{0}^{1}d\tau\,\big[s(1-\tau)-\frac{(s-\tau)^{2}}{2}\chi(\tau\leq s)\big]\\
&\hskip 28.45274pt\times\sum_{r,q,k,p\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}_{N}(r)A_{q,k}^{(6)}A^{(6)}_{q+r,k}A^{(3)}_{p,k}A^{(6)}_{p,k}\big\langle\xi_{\tau},\big(a_{p}^{*}a_{p}+a_{k}^{*}a_{k}+a_{p+k}^{*}a_{p+k}\big)\xi_{\tau}\big\rangle\end{split}

in Fourier space. With the bounds in Lemma
9
, Lemma
15
, Lemma
19
, we find that
|\widetilde{T}_{0}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}
and
|\widetilde{T}_{2}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1-\beta/4+\epsilon}
, for any
\epsilon>0
. Choosing
\epsilon>0
small enough, we conclude that
|\widetilde{S}_{1,3,2}|\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}
and thus that

\langle\xi_{s},T_{3}\xi_{s}\rangle\leq s\int dxdy\,V_{N}(y)|\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)|^{2}+CN^{4\kappa-1}

for all
s\in[0;1]
. Combining this bound with (
166
), (
167
), (
168
), we conclude that

\begin{split}\langle&e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{V}_{N}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&\leq\frac{t^{2}}{2}\int dxdy\,V_{N}(y)|\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)|^{2}+CN^{-\beta+\epsilon}\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{W}_{N}\xi_{s}\rangle+CN^{4\kappa-1}.\end{split}

(169)

Similarly, replacing
V_{N}
with
|V_{N}|
(and observing that all estimates only depend on
\|V_{N}\|_{1}
,
\|\hat{V}_{N}\|_{2}
), we obtain

\begin{split}\langle&e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,\mathcal{W}_{N}e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&\leq\frac{t^{2}}{2}\int dxdy\,|V_{N}(y)||\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)|^{2}+CN^{-\beta+\epsilon}\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{W}_{N}\xi_{s}\rangle+CN^{4\kappa-1}.\end{split}

(170)

With
\|\widehat{|V|}\|_{\infty}\leq C\|V\|_{1}\leq C
, we find

\Big|\int dxdy|V_{N}(y)||\check{A}^{(6)}(y,x)|^{2}\Big|=\Big|N^{-1+\kappa}\sum_{r,p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\widehat{|V|}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\,A^{(6)}_{p,q}A^{(6)}_{p+r,q}\Big|\leq CN^{5\kappa/2}.

Choosing
\epsilon>0
small enough, (
170
) implies that

\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{W}_{N}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq CN^{5\kappa/2}+C\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{W}_{N}\xi_{s}\rangle

for all
t\in[0;1]
. By Gronwall’s Lemma, we conclude that
\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{W}_{N}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq CN^{5\kappa/2}

for all
t\in[0;1]
. Inserting this bound in (
169
) and observing that
5\kappa/2-\beta=4\kappa-1-\beta/2
, we obtain (
165
).
∎

Finally, we evaluate the expectation of the kinetic energy operator in our trial state
\xi=e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
.

Lemma 26
.

We have

\begin{split}\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }&\Omega,\mathcal{K}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&=-N^{1/2}\int dxdy\,N^{2-2\kappa}(Vf)(N^{1-\kappa}x)\check{\sigma}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(x,y)+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Proof.

We use the notation
\xi_{t}=e^{tA^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega
. Since
\langle\Omega,\mathcal{K}\Omega\rangle=0
, we have

\begin{split}\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{K}\xi_{t}\rangle=\;&2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},[\mathcal{K},A^{*}\Theta]\xi_{t}\rangle\\
=\;&2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle+2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},A^{*}[\mathcal{K},\Theta]\xi_{s}\rangle.\end{split}

(171)

Expressing the cubic operator
A^{*}
in momentum space, as

A^{*}=\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}A_{p,q}a_{p+q}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}a_{-q}^{*}\,,

and recalling the definition (
62
) of the coefficients
A_{p,q}
, we find

\begin{split}[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]&=2N^{-1/2}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}p^{2}\eta_{p}\sigma_{q}a_{p+q}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}a_{-q}^{*}\\
&=-N^{\kappa-1/2}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\tilde{\chi}(p)\sigma_{q}\,a_{p+q}^{*}a_{-p}^{*}a_{-q}^{*}.\end{split}

(172)

With Lemma
11
, it follows that

2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle=2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]\xi_{s}\rangle+O(N^{4\kappa-1})

(173)

if we choose the parameter
\alpha>0
in the definition of the cutoff
\Theta
large enough. Moreover, from (
172
), we obtain
\|(\mathcal{N}+1)^{-3/2}[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]\|\leq CN^{1+\kappa/4}
. From Lemma
11
, this implies the rough but useful a-priori bound

\Big|\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]\Theta\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\leq CN^{5\kappa/2+1}.

(174)

Let us now consider the second term on the r.h.s. of (
171
). We observe that the cutoff
\Theta
preserves the number of particles and, on the
m
-particle sector of the Fock space
\mathcal{F}=\oplus_{m=0}^{\infty}L^{2}_{s}(\Lambda^{m})
, it acts as multiplication operator with the function

F_{m}(x_{1},\dots,x_{m})=\Theta\Big(\int_{\Lambda}dw\,\big(\sum_{j=1}^{m}\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x_{j}-w)+1\big)^{n}/N^{\alpha}\Big).

Since also the kinetic energy operator
\mathcal{K}
leaves the number of particles invariant, on the
m
-particle sector, the action of the commutator
[\mathcal{K},\Theta]
is given by

\begin{split}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\big[&-\Delta_{x_{j}},F_{m}(x_{1},\dots,x_{m})\big]\\
&=-2\sum_{j=1}^{m}(\nabla_{x_{j}}F_{m})(x_{1},\dots,x_{m})\cdot\nabla_{x_{j}}-\sum_{j=1}^{m}(\Delta_{x_{j}}F_{m})(x_{1},\dots,x_{m}).\end{split}

(175)

We compute

\begin{split}\nabla_{x_{j}}F_{m}(x_{1},\dots,x_{m})=\;&nN^{-\alpha}\Theta^{\prime}\Big(\int_{\Lambda}dw\,\big(\sum_{j=1}^{m}\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x_{j}-w)+1\big)^{n}/N^{\alpha}\Big)\\
&\times\int_{\Lambda}dw\,\big(\sum_{j=1}^{m}\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x_{j}-w)+1\big)^{n-1}\nabla\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x_{j}-w).\end{split}

The fact that
\Theta^{\prime}
is supported in the interval
[1;2]
implies, on the one hand, that

[\mathcal{K},\Theta]=\mathbbm{1}_{[1;\infty)}(\mathcal{L}_{n}/N^{\alpha})[\mathcal{K},\Theta]

and, on the other hand, the bound

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{j}}F_{m}(x_{1},\dots,x_{m})|^{2}\leq\;&n^{2}N^{-2\alpha}\Big|\Theta^{\prime}\Big(\int_{\Lambda}dw\,\big(\sum_{j=1}^{m}\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x_{j}-w)+1\big)^{n}/N^{\alpha}\Big)\Big|^{2}\\
&\times\Big|\int_{\Lambda}dw\big(\sum_{j=1}^{m}\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x_{j}-w)+1\big)^{n-1}|\nabla\chi_{\ell_{B}}(x_{j}-w)|\Big|^{2}\\
\leq\;&C\|\nabla\chi_{\ell_{B}}\|_{\infty}^{2}\leq C\ell_{B}^{-2}\end{split}

for a constant
C>0
(depending on the parameter
n\in\mathbb{N}
). Similarly, we find

|\Delta_{x_{j}}F_{m}(x_{1},\dots,x_{m})|\leq C\ell_{B}^{-2}.

From (
175
), we conclude that

\|\frac{1}{(\mathcal{N}+1)}[\mathcal{K},\Theta]\xi_{s}\|\leq C\ell_{B}^{-2}\|(\mathcal{K}+1)^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|

for every
s\in[0;1]
and therefore that

\Big|\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},A^{*}[\mathcal{K},\Theta]\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\leq C\ell_{B}^{-2}\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\|(\mathcal{N}+1)\mathbbm{1}_{[1;\infty)}(\mathcal{L}_{n}/N^{\alpha})A\xi_{s}\|\|(\mathcal{K}+1)^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|.

With Lemma
11
we obtain that, for any
\nu>0
,

\Big|\int_{0}^{t}ds\,\langle\xi_{s},A^{*}[\mathcal{K},\Theta]\xi_{s}\rangle\Big|\leq CN^{-\nu}\int_{0}^{t}ds\|(\mathcal{K}+1)^{1/2}\xi_{s}\|

(176)

for every
t\in[0;1]
, if
\alpha>0
is large enough. Inserting the last bound together with (
174
) in (
171
), we arrive at

\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{K}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq CN^{5\kappa/2+1}+CN^{-\nu}\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},\mathcal{K}\xi_{s}\rangle

for every
t\in[0;1]
. By Gronwall’s Lemma, we obtain
\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{K}\xi_{t}\rangle\leq CN^{5\kappa/2+1}
for all
t\in[0;1]
. Plugging this back into (
176
), we conclude from (
171
) and (
173
) that

\langle\xi_{t},\mathcal{K}\xi_{t}\rangle=2\text{Re }\int_{0}^{t}ds\langle\xi_{s},[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]\xi_{s}\rangle+O(N^{4\kappa-1})

(177)

if the parameter
\alpha>0
is large enough. Translating (
172
) to position space, we find

\begin{split}[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]=\;&-\sqrt{N}\int dxdydz\,N^{2-2\kappa}(Vf)(N^{1-\kappa}(x-y))\check{\sigma}(x-z)a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}\\
&+\int dxdydz\,\check{A}_{\mathcal{K}}^{\text{low}}(x-y,x-z)\,a_{x}^{*}a_{y}^{*}a_{z}^{*}\end{split}

(178)

with
A_{\mathcal{K}}^{\text{low}}
as defined in (
83
). To bound the second term, with the kernel
A_{\mathcal{K}}^{\text{low}}
, we apply Lemma
14
; using the bounds
\|A_{\mathcal{K}}^{\text{low}}\|_{2}\leq CN^{(5\kappa-1)/2}
and

\sum_{u,v\in\Lambda_{B}}\|\check{A}^{\text{low}}_{\mathcal{K},u,v}\|_{2}\leq CN^{(5\kappa-1)/2+\epsilon}

from Lemma
10
, we conclude that this contribution is negligible, of the order
N^{4\kappa-1}
. As for the first term on the r.h.s. of (
178
), we compare it with (
150
) and we observe that it has exactly the same form as the cubic operator
\mathcal{C}_{N}
, just with the prefactor
N_{0}^{1/2}
replacing by
N^{1/2}
and with the potential
V
replaced by
Vf
. Hence, proceeding exactly as in Lemma
24
, we conclude that

2\text{Re }\langle\xi_{s},[\mathcal{K},A^{*}]\xi_{s}\rangle=-2sN^{1/2}\int dxdy\,N^{2-2\kappa}(Vf)(N^{1-\kappa}x)\check{\sigma}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(x,y)+O(N^{4\kappa-1})

and therefore, from (
177
), that

\begin{split}\langle e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega,&\mathcal{K}e^{A^{*}\Theta-\text{h.c.}\ }\Omega\rangle\\
&=-N^{1/2}\int dxdy\,N^{2-2\kappa}(Vf)(N^{1-\kappa}x)\check{\sigma}(y)\check{A}^{(6)}(x,y)+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\,.\end{split}

∎

3.4
Proof of Theorem
2

We combine the results of Lemma
24
, Lemma
25
and Lemma
26
.

Lemma 27
.

Let
\mathcal{C}_{N}
be the cubic operator defined in (
107
),
\mathcal{V}_{N}
the quartic potential energy operator and
\mathcal{K}
the quadratic kinetic energy operator. Then, we have

\begin{split}\langle\xi,(\mathcal{K}+&\mathcal{C}_{N}+\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*}+\mathcal{V}_{N})\xi\rangle\\
=\;&N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\eta_{\infty,p}+\eta_{\infty,p+q})\sigma_{q}^{2}\\
&-N^{4\kappa-1}\sum_{|p|,|q|\geq N^{\kappa/2}}(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\frac{\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\,\widehat{Vf}(q/N^{1-\kappa})\,\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})}{4p^{2}q^{2}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}\\
&+N^{4\kappa-1}\sum_{|p|,|q|\geq N^{\kappa/2}}(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\frac{(p\cdot q)\,\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\,\widehat{Vf}(q/N^{1-\kappa})^{2}}{4q^{4}p^{2}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Proof.

From Lemma
24
and Lemma
26
, we find, writing all contributions in momentum space

\begin{split}\langle\xi,(\mathcal{K}+\mathcal{C}_{N}+\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*})\xi\rangle&=\;N^{\kappa-1/2}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\big(2\widehat{Vw}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\big)\sigma_{q}\\
&\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+A_{p,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p})\\
&+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(179)

Note that we have replaced
N_{0}
by
N
in the contribution from
\mathcal{C}_{N}
using that
\lvert N_{0}^{1/2}-N^{1/2}\rvert\leq N^{3\kappa/2-1}
and that the whole term is of order
N^{5\kappa/2}
, see Lemma
5
and
7
.
As for the expectation of
\mathcal{V}_{N}
, we find, from Lemma
25
,

\langle\xi,\mathcal{V}_{N}\xi\rangle=\frac{N^{\kappa-1}}{2}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})A^{(6)}_{p,q}A^{(6)}_{p-r,q}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).

Since
A^{(6)}_{p,q}=A^{(6)}_{-p-q,q}
, we have

\begin{split}\frac{N^{\kappa-1}}{2}&\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})A^{(6)}_{p,q}A^{(6)}_{p-r,q}\\
=\;&N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(A_{p-r,q}+A_{q,p-r}+A_{-p-q+r,p-r})A^{(6)}_{p,q}\\
=\;&N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(A_{p-r,q}+A_{q,p-r})\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+A_{p,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p})\\
&+N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})A_{p,q}\\
&\hskip 5.69046pt\times(A_{q+r,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,q+r}+A_{p-r,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p-r}+A_{p-r,q+r}+A_{q+r,p-r}).\end{split}

The last term corresponds to the contribution proportional to
A_{-p-q+r,p-r}
in the first line, after switching to new variables
-p-q+r\to p,p-r\to q
. Rewriting

\begin{split}\sum_{p,q,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})&A_{p.q}(A_{q+r,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,q+r}+A_{p-r,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p-r})\\
&=\sum_{p,q,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p})(A_{p-r,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p-r})\\
&=\sum_{p,q,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(A_{p-r,q}+A_{q,p-r})(A_{p,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p})\end{split}

we conclude that

\begin{split}\langle\xi,\mathcal{V}_{N}\xi\rangle=\;&N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(A_{p-r,q}+A_{q,p-r})\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+2A_{p,-p-q}+2A_{-p-q,p})\\
&+N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})A_{p,q}(A_{p-r,q+r}+A_{q+r,p-r})+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Next, we observe that

\begin{split}A_{p-r,q}&+A_{q,p-r}=N^{-1/2}\eta_{p-r}\sigma_{q}-N^{-1/2}\frac{2(p-r)\cdot q}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}\sigma_{q}\eta_{p-r}\\
+\;&N^{-1/2}\frac{2q^{2}\eta_{q}(\sigma_{p-r}-\eta_{p-r})}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}+N^{-1/2}\frac{2q^{2}\eta_{p-r}(\eta_{q}-\sigma_{q})}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}.\\
&\end{split}

(180)

Hence, we can decompose

\langle\xi,\mathcal{V}_{N}\xi\rangle=\sum_{j=1}^{5}C_{\mathcal{V},j}+O(N^{4\kappa-1})

where

\begin{split}C_{\mathcal{V},1}&=N^{\kappa-3/2}\sum\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{p-r}\sigma_{q}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+2A_{p,-p-q}+2A_{-p-q,p})\\
C_{\mathcal{V},2}&=-N^{\kappa-3/2}\sum\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2(p-r)\cdot q}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}\eta_{p-r}\sigma_{q}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+2A_{p,-p-q}+2A_{-p-q,p})\\
C_{\mathcal{V},3}&=N^{\kappa-3/2}\sum\frac{2q^{2}(\eta_{q}-\sigma_{q})\eta_{p-r}}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+2A_{p,-p-q}+2A_{-p-q,p})\\
C_{\mathcal{V},4}&=N^{\kappa-3/2}\sum\frac{2q^{2}\eta_{q}(\sigma_{p-r}-\eta_{p-r})}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+2A_{p,-p-q}+2A_{-p-q,p})\\
C_{\mathcal{V},5}&=N^{\kappa-1}\sum\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})A_{p,q}(A_{p-r,q+r}+A_{q+r,p-r}).\end{split}

(181)

The last three terms are negligible. In fact, with the bounds in Lemma
7
, we find

\begin{split}\lvert C_{\mathcal{V},5}\rvert&\leq CN^{5\kappa-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q,r\in\Lambda^{*}\colon\\
p,q,p+r,q-r\neq 0\end{subarray}}\lvert\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\rvert\lvert p\rvert^{-2}\lvert p+r\rvert^{-2}\lvert q\rvert^{-2}\lvert q-r\rvert^{-2}\\
&\leq CN^{5\kappa-2}\sum_{r\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\lvert\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\rvert\lvert r\rvert^{-2}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.\end{split}

Moreover,

\begin{split}\lvert C_{\mathcal{V},4}\rvert&\leq CN^{\kappa-2}\lVert\eta-\sigma\rVert_{1}(\lVert\eta\rVert_{2}^{2}+\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2}^{2})(\lVert\eta\rVert_{1}+\lVert\sigma\rVert_{1})\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}\end{split}

and

\begin{split}\lvert C_{\mathcal{V},3}\rvert&\leq CN^{\kappa-2}\lVert\eta/\lvert\cdot\rvert^{2}\rVert\lVert\lvert\cdot\rvert^{2}(\eta-\sigma)\rVert_{2}(\lVert\eta\rVert_{2}+\lVert\sigma\rVert_{2})(\lVert\eta\rVert_{1}+\lVert\sigma\rVert_{1})\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.\end{split}

Next, we consider the term
C_{\mathcal{V},1}
. From Lemma
3
, we have

\begin{split}\Big|\sum_{r\in\Lambda^{*}}N^{\kappa-1}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\eta_{\infty,p-r}+N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vw}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\Big|&\leq CN^{2\kappa-1}\\
\Big|\sum_{r\in\Lambda^{*}}N^{\kappa-1}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})(\eta_{p-r}-\eta_{\infty,p-r})\Big|&\leq CN^{5\kappa/2-1}.\end{split}

This implies that

\begin{split}C_{\mathcal{V},1}=\;&-N^{\kappa-1/2}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\widehat{Vw}\,(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{q}\\
&\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}+2A_{p,-p-q}+2A_{-p-q,p})+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

With (
179
), we obtain

\begin{split}\langle\xi,(\mathcal{K}+\mathcal{C}_{N}&+\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*})\xi\rangle+C_{\mathcal{V},1}\\
=\;&N^{\kappa-1/2}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{q}(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q})\\
&+N^{\kappa-1/2}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa}))\sigma_{q}(A_{p,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p})+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
=\;&C_{1}+C_{2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(182)

Expanding
A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}
and
A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q}
similarly to (
180
) and observing that the contribution of all terms containing the difference
\sigma-\eta
are negligible (similarly as
C_{\mathcal{V},3},C_{\mathcal{V}_{4}}
above), we conclude that

\begin{split}C_{1}=\;&-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma_{q}^{2}\Big(\frac{2\eta_{p}\,p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}-\frac{2\eta_{p+q}(p+q)\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\Big)\\
&+N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma^{2}_{q}(\eta_{p}+\eta_{p+q})+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Switching
p+q\to-p
in the second term on the first line and using that
\|\eta-\eta_{\infty}\|_{1}\leq CN^{3\kappa/2}
in the second line, we conclude that

\begin{split}C_{1}=\;&-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\big(\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\hat{V}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})\big)\frac{2\eta_{p}\sigma_{q}^{2}\,p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\\
&+N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\sigma^{2}_{q}(\eta_{\infty,p}+\eta_{\infty,p+q})+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(183)

As for
C_{2}
, we expand
A_{p,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p}
similarly to (
180
) and we observe that contributions proportional to
\eta-\sigma
are negligible (similarly as
C_{\mathcal{V},3},C_{\mathcal{V}_{4}}
above) and that the remaining terms can be combined into

\begin{split}-&N^{-1/2}\frac{2p\cdot(-p-q)}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\sigma_{p+q}+N^{-1/2}\eta_{p}\sigma_{p+q}\\
&=2N^{-1/2}\frac{p^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\eta_{p+q}+2N^{-1/2}\frac{p^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}(\sigma_{p+q}-\eta_{p+q}).\end{split}

Since also the contribution of the last term, proportional to
\sigma_{p+q}-\eta_{p+q}
, is negligible, we arrive at

\begin{split}C_{2}&=N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2p^{2}+2(p+q)^{2}}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\sigma_{q}\eta_{p}\eta_{p+q}+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
&=N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\frac{2(p+q)^{2}\eta_{p+q}\eta_{p}\sigma_{q}}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Again, we can replace
\sigma_{q}
by
\eta_{q}
, with a negligible error. Also, using the definition (
63
) for replacing
2(p+q)^{2}\eta_{p+q}
by
-N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})
and then removing the momentum restriction on
p+q
produces contributions of order
N^{4\kappa-1}
. Similarly, we can replace the smooth cutoff contained in
\eta
by a sharp one at
N^{\kappa/2}
noting that the difference is supported on momenta between
N^{\kappa/2}
and
2N^{\kappa/2}
.
We conclude that

\begin{split}C_{2}&=-N^{4\kappa-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\colon\\
|p|,|q|\geq N^{\kappa/2}\end{subarray}}(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\frac{\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\widehat{Vf}(q/N^{1-\kappa})}{4p^{2}q^{2}\big(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}\big)}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(184)

Next, we consider the term
C_{\mathcal{V},2}
, from (
181
). We start by observing that

\begin{split}\Big|N^{\kappa-3/2}&\sum_{p,q,r}\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2(p-r)\cdot q}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}\eta_{p-r}\sigma_{q}(A_{p,-p-q}+A_{-p-q,p})\Big|\\
&\leq CN^{\kappa-3/2}\sum_{p,q,r}\frac{|r||q|}{r^{2}+q^{2}+(r+q)^{2}}|\eta_{r}||\sigma_{q}|(|A_{p,-p-q}|+|A_{-p-q,p}|)\\
&\leq CN^{4\kappa-2}\sum_{p,q,r}\frac{|r||\eta_{r}|}{q^{2}(q+r)^{2}}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}\end{split}

where we applied (
71
), from Lemma
7
. Thus, we have

\begin{split}C_{\mathcal{V},2}=\;&-N^{\kappa-3/2}\sum\hat{V}(r/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2(p-r)\cdot q}{(p-r)^{2}+q^{2}+(p-r+q)^{2}}\eta_{p-r}\sigma_{q}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times(A_{p,q}+A_{q,p}+A_{-p-q,q}+A_{q,-p-q})+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
=\;&-N^{\kappa-3/2}\sum\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\sigma_{q}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times(A_{r,q}+A_{q,r}+A_{-r-q,q}+A_{q,-r-q})+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\end{split}

after switching
p-r\to p,r\to p
. We expand
A_{r,q}+A_{q,r}
and
A_{-r-q,q}+A_{q,-r-q}
similarly as in (
180
) and, as usual, we observe that contributions containing the difference
\sigma-\eta
are negligible. Thus, we arrive at

\begin{split}C_{\mathcal{V},2}=\;&-N^{\kappa-2}\sum(\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})+\hat{V}((p+q-r)/N^{1-\kappa}))\frac{2p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\eta_{r}\sigma^{2}_{q}\\
&+N^{\kappa-2}\sum\big(\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})+\hat{V}((p+q-r)/N^{1-\kappa})\big)\\
&\hskip 71.13188pt\times\frac{2p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\frac{2r\cdot q}{r^{2}+q^{2}+(r+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\eta_{r}\sigma^{2}_{q}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Let us consider the second term, for example the contribution proportional to
\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})
(the contribution proportional to
\hat{V}((p+q-r)/N^{1-\kappa})
can be treated analogously). With the change of variable
r\to-r
, we can write

\begin{split}N^{\kappa-2}&\sum\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\frac{2r\cdot q}{r^{2}+q^{2}+(r+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\eta_{r}\sigma^{2}_{q}\\
=\;&\frac{N^{\kappa-2}}{2}\sum\big(\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})-\hat{V}((p+r)/N^{1-\kappa})\big)\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\frac{2p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\frac{2r\cdot q}{r^{2}+q^{2}+(r+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\eta_{r}\sigma^{2}_{q}\\
&-N^{\kappa-2}\sum\hat{V}((p+r)/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\\
&\hskip 85.35826pt\times\frac{(2r\cdot q)^{2}}{\big(r^{2}+q^{2}+(r+q)^{2}\big)\big(r^{2}+q^{2}+(r-q)^{2}\big)}\eta_{p}\eta_{r}\sigma^{2}_{q}.\end{split}

In the first term, we bound
|\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})-\hat{V}((p+r)/N^{1-\kappa})|\leq C|r|/N^{1-\kappa}
and, using
\|\eta\|_{1}\leq CN
,

\begin{split}N^{2\kappa-3}\sum_{p,q,r}&\frac{|p||r|^{2}|q|^{2}|\eta_{p}||\eta_{r}||\sigma_{q}|^{2}}{(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})(r^{2}+q^{2}+(r+q)^{2})}\\
&\hskip 11.38092pt\leq CN^{2\kappa-2}\sum_{p,q}\frac{|p||q|^{2}|\eta_{p}||\sigma_{q}|^{2}}{(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}\leq CN^{4\kappa-2}\sum_{p,q}\frac{|p||\eta_{p}|}{(p+q)^{2}q^{2}}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.\end{split}

In the second term we proceed similarly, using
|r|^{2}|\eta_{r}|\leq CN^{\kappa}
and
\sum_{r}|r|^{-2}|r+q|^{2}\leq C|q|^{-1}
.
We conclude that

N^{\kappa-2}\sum\hat{V}((p-r)/N^{1-\kappa})\frac{2p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\frac{2r\cdot q}{r^{2}+q^{2}+(r+q)^{2}}\eta_{p}\eta_{r}\sigma^{2}_{q}=O(N^{4\kappa-1})

and thus that

\begin{split}C_{\mathcal{V},2}=\;&N^{\kappa-1}\sum(\widehat{Vw}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vw}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\frac{2\eta_{p}\sigma^{2}_{q}\,p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Combined with (
182
), (
183
), (
184
), we obtain

\begin{split}\langle\xi,(\mathcal{K}&+\mathcal{C}_{N}+\mathcal{C}_{N}^{*}+\mathcal{V}_{N})\xi\rangle\\
=\;&N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\hat{V}(p/N^{1-\kappa})(\eta_{\infty,p}+\eta_{\infty,p+q})\sigma_{q}^{2}\\
&-N^{4\kappa-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\colon\\
|p|,|q|>N^{\kappa/2}\end{subarray}}(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\\
&\hskip 56.9055pt\times\frac{\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\widehat{Vf}(q/N^{1-\kappa})}{4p^{2}q^{2}\big(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}\big)}\\
&-N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\big(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})\big)\frac{2\eta_{p}\sigma_{q}^{2}\,p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(185)

We can replace
\eta_{p}
by
\eta_{\infty,p}\mathbbm{1}(\lvert\cdot\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2})
, because

N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{|p|<2N^{\kappa/2},q\in\Lambda^{*}}\frac{|p||q||\eta_{\infty,p}||\sigma_{q}|^{2}}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\leq N^{5\kappa/2-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\frac{|q||\sigma_{q}|^{2}}{p^{2}(p+q)^{2}}\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}.

Similarly, we can also replace
\sigma_{q}^{2}
by
\eta_{\infty,q}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\lvert\cdot\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2})
. This follows from a symmetrization using a change of variable
q\to-q
, because

\begin{split}N^{\kappa-1}&\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\colon\lvert p\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2}}\frac{|p\cdot q|^{2}|\eta_{\infty,p}|\lvert\sigma_{q}^{2}-\eta_{\infty,q}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\lvert\cdot\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2})\rvert}{(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p-q)^{2})}\\
&\leq N^{2\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\colon\lvert p\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2}}\frac{|q|^{2}\lvert\sigma_{q}^{2}-\eta_{\infty,q}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\lvert\cdot\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2})\rvert}{p^{4}}\\
&\leq N^{5\kappa/2-1}\sum_{q\in\Lambda^{*}}\lvert\sigma_{q}-\eta_{\infty,q}\mathbbm{1}(\lvert\cdot\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2})\rvert\leq CN^{4\kappa-1}\end{split}

and

\begin{split}&N^{2\kappa-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\colon\lvert p\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2}\end{subarray}}\frac{|p||q|^{2}|\eta_{\infty,p}|\lvert\sigma_{q}^{2}-\eta_{\infty,q}^{2}\mathbbm{1}(\lvert\cdot\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2})\rvert}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\\
&\leq N^{3\kappa-2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\colon\lvert p\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2}\end{subarray}}\frac{|\eta_{\infty,p}|\lvert\sigma_{q}-\eta_{\infty,q}\mathbbm{1}(\lvert\cdot\rvert\geq N^{\kappa/2})\rvert}{\lvert p\rvert}\leq C\log(N)N^{4\kappa-1-\beta/2}.\end{split}

We can therefore rewrite the last term on the r.h.s. of (
185
) as

\begin{split}-&N^{\kappa-1}\sum_{p,q\in\Lambda^{*}}\big(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})\big)\frac{2\eta_{p}\sigma_{q}^{2}\,p\cdot q}{p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2}}\\
&=N^{4\kappa-1}\sum_{|p|,|q|\geq N^{\kappa/2}}\big(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})\big)\\
&\hskip 113.81102pt\times\frac{(p\cdot q)\,\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\widehat{Vf}(q/N^{1-\kappa})^{2}}{4p^{2}q^{4}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\end{split}

concluding the proof of the lemma.
∎

We are now ready to show Theorem
2
.

Proof of Theorem
2
.

Combining Lemma
13
with Lemma
15
, Lemma
18
, Lemma
19
, Lemma
20
and Lemma
27
, we obtain, for any
\kappa\geq 1/2
,

\begin{split}\langle\psi_{N},&\mathcal{H}_{N}\psi_{N}\rangle\\
=\;&4\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{1+\kappa}\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}}\Big(\sqrt{\lvert p\rvert^{4}+2p^{2}N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{p}{N^{1-\kappa}})}-p^{2}-N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{p}{N^{1-\kappa}})+\frac{N^{2\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{p}{N^{1-\kappa}})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}\Big)\\
&-N^{4\kappa-1}\sum_{|p|,|q|\geq N^{\kappa/2}}(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\frac{\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\,\widehat{Vf}(q/N^{1-\kappa})\,\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa})}{4p^{2}q^{2}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}\\
&+N^{4\kappa-1}\sum_{|p|,|q|\geq N^{\kappa/2}}(\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})+\widehat{Vf}((p+q)/N^{1-\kappa}))\\
&\hskip 142.26378pt\times\frac{(p\cdot q)\,\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})\,\widehat{Vf}(q/N^{1-\kappa})^{2}}{4q^{4}p^{2}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
=:\;&4\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{1+\kappa}+S_{1}+S_{2}+S_{3}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

(186)

Let us consider the term
S_{1}
first. Since the summand decays as
N^{3\kappa}|p|^{-4}
, as
|p|\to\infty
, we can restrict the sum to
|p|\leq N^{1-\kappa}
, up to a negligible error. We define

g_{p}(t)=\sqrt{p^{4}+2p^{2}N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{tp}{N^{1-\kappa}})}-p^{2}-N^{\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{tp}{N^{1-\kappa}})+\frac{N^{2\kappa}\widehat{Vf}(\frac{tp}{N^{1-\kappa}})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}.

Then

\begin{split}S_{1}=&\;\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}\\
\lvert p\rvert\leq N^{1-\kappa}\end{subarray}}\left(\sqrt{|p|^{4}+16\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}p^{2}}-p^{2}-8\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}+\frac{N^{2\kappa}(8\pi\mathfrak{a})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}\right)\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{s}dt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}\\
\lvert p\rvert\leq N^{1-\kappa}\end{subarray}}\frac{d^{2}}{dt^{2}}g_{p}(t)+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\end{split}

where we used
\widehat{Vf}(0)=8\pi\mathfrak{a}
and
g^{\prime}_{p}(0)=0
, because
\int xV(x)f(x)dx=0
by symmetry. Explicit computation of the derivatives of
g_{p}
leads to the estimate

\lvert g_{p}^{\prime\prime}(t)\rvert\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}CN^{4\kappa-2}&\textrm{ if }\lvert p\rvert\leq KN^{\kappa/2}\\
CN^{4\kappa-2}\frac{N^{\kappa}}{\lvert p\rvert^{2}}&\textrm{ if }\lvert p\rvert>KN^{\kappa/2}\end{array}\right.

if
K>0
is large enough. Hence,

\begin{split}S_{1}&=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}\end{subarray}}\left(\sqrt{p^{4}+16\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}p^{2}}-p^{2}-8\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}+\frac{N^{2\kappa}(8\pi\mathfrak{a})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}\right)+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Defining

h(p)=\sqrt{p^{4}+16\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}p^{2}}-p^{2}-8\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}+\frac{N^{2\kappa}(8\pi\mathfrak{a})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}

we can write

\begin{split}S_{1}=\;&\frac{1}{2(2\pi)^{3}}\int dp^{3}\left(\sqrt{p^{4}+16\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}p^{2}}-p^{2}-8\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}+\frac{N^{2\kappa}(8\pi\mathfrak{a})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}\right)\\
&+\frac{1}{2(2\pi)^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}\end{subarray}}\int_{[-\pi,\pi]^{3}}d\zeta\,(h(p)-h(p+\zeta))+O(N^{2\kappa})+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
\end{split}

where the error
O(N^{2\kappa})
arises from the integral around
p=0
. By explicit computation, we find

\begin{split}\Big|\frac{d^{2}}{ds^{2}}h(p+s\zeta)\Big|\leq C\frac{N^{2\kappa}}{\lvert p+s\zeta\rvert^{4}}.\end{split}

Thus,

\begin{split}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}\end{subarray}}\int_{[-\pi,\pi]^{3}}d\zeta\,(h(p)-h(p+\zeta))&=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}\end{subarray}}\int_{[-\pi,\pi]^{3}}d\zeta\int_{0}^{1}ds\,\frac{d}{ds}h(p+s\zeta)\\
&=-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p\in\Lambda^{*}_{+}\end{subarray}}\int_{[-\pi,\pi]^{3}}d\zeta\int_{0}^{1}ds\int_{0}^{s}\,\frac{d^{2}}{dt^{2}}h(p+t\zeta)=O(N^{2\kappa})\end{split}

because
(d/ds)h(p+s\zeta)
, evaluated at
s=0
, is an odd function of
p
(and therefore its sum over
p
vanishes). We conclude that, for
\kappa\geq 1/2
,

\begin{split}S_{1}&=\frac{1}{2(2\pi)^{3}}\int dp^{3}\left(\sqrt{p^{4}+16\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}p^{2}}-p^{2}-8\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa}+\frac{N^{2\kappa}(8\pi\mathfrak{a})^{2}}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}\right)+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
&=\frac{(8\pi\mathfrak{a}N^{\kappa})^{5/2}}{2(2\pi)^{3}}\int dp^{3}\left(\sqrt{p^{4}+2p^{2}}-p^{2}-1+\frac{1}{2\lvert p\rvert^{2}}\right)+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
&=\frac{512\sqrt{\pi}}{15}\mathfrak{a}^{5/2}N^{5\kappa/2}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Next, we consider the term
S_{2}
, as defined in (
186
). Since

\begin{split}\sum_{|p|>N^{1-\kappa},|q|\leq N^{1-\kappa}}\frac{1}{|p|^{4}|q|^{2}}&\leq C,\\
\sum_{|p|,|q|>N^{1-\kappa}}|\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})|\frac{1}{p^{2}|q|^{4}}&\leq CN^{-1+\kappa}\|\widehat{Vf}\|_{2}\|\chi(|.|>N^{1-\kappa})/|.|^{2}\|_{2}\leq C\end{split}

we can restrict the sum to
|p|,|q|\leq N^{1-\kappa}
. Estimating
|\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})-8\pi\mathfrak{a}|\leq Cp^{2}/N^{2-2\kappa}
, we obtain

S_{2}=-2048N^{4\kappa-1}\pi^{4}\mathfrak{a}^{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\\
N^{\kappa/2}\leq\lvert p\rvert,\lvert q\rvert\leq N^{1-\kappa}\end{subarray}}\frac{1}{p^{2}q^{2}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).

As for the term
S_{3}
, we can proceed as we did for
S_{2}
to restrict the sum to
|q|\leq N^{1-\kappa}
. In order to restrict it to
|p|\leq N^{1-\kappa}
, on the other hand, we need additionally a symmetrization argument (replacing
q
with
-q
). This argument is very similar to
[
9
, Eq. (2.39)]
; we skip the details. After restricting to
|p|,|q|\leq N^{1-\kappa}
, we can use
|\widehat{Vf}(p/N^{1-\kappa})-8\pi\mathfrak{a}|\leq Cp^{2}/N^{2-2\kappa}
as we did for
S_{2}
, to conclude that

S_{3}=2048N^{4\kappa-1}\pi^{4}\mathfrak{a}^{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\\
N^{\kappa/2}\leq\lvert p\rvert,\lvert q\rvert\leq N^{1-\kappa}\end{subarray}}\frac{p\cdot q}{q^{4}p^{2}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).

We arrive at

S_{2}+S_{3}=2048\,N^{4\kappa-1}\pi^{4}\mathfrak{a}^{4}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}p,q\in\Lambda^{*}\\
N^{\kappa/2}\leq\lvert p\rvert,\lvert q\rvert\leq N^{1-\kappa}\end{subarray}}\frac{p\cdot q-q^{2}}{q^{4}p^{2}(p^{2}+q^{2}+(p+q)^{2})}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).

Proceeding exactly as in
[
9
, Section 2]
, we can replace the sum by an integral, with only a negligible error. We find

\begin{split}S_{2}+S_{3}=\;&\frac{16\mathfrak{a}^{4}}{\pi^{2}}\,N^{4\kappa-1}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}N^{\kappa/2}\leq\lvert p\rvert,\lvert q\rvert\leq N^{1-\kappa}\end{subarray}}dpdq\frac{p\cdot q-q^{2}}{p^{2}q^{4}(p^{2}+q^{2}+p\cdot q)}\\
&+O(N^{2\kappa-\beta/2})+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

Rescaling the integration variables,
p,q\to p/N^{\kappa/2},q/N^{\kappa/2}
, we obtain

\begin{split}S_{2}+S_{3}=\frac{16\mathfrak{a}^{4}}{\pi^{2}}\,N^{4\kappa-1}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq\lvert p\rvert,\lvert q\rvert\leq N^{\beta/2}\end{subarray}}dpdq\frac{p\cdot q-q^{2}}{p^{2}q^{4}(p^{2}+q^{2}+p\cdot q)}+O(N^{4\kappa-1}).\end{split}

for
\kappa\geq 1/2
. Up to an error of order
N^{4\kappa-1}
, we can remove the restrictions on
p
. By explicit computation, we conclude that

\begin{split}S_{2}+S_{3}&=\frac{16\mathfrak{a}^{4}}{\pi^{2}}\,N^{4\kappa-1}\int_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq\lvert q\rvert\leq N^{\beta/2}\end{subarray}}dpdq\frac{p\cdot q-q^{2}}{p^{2}q^{4}(p^{2}+q^{2}+p\cdot q)}+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\\
&=-32\pi\left(\frac{4\pi}{3}-\sqrt{3}\right)\mathfrak{a}^{4}N^{4\kappa-1}\log(N^{\beta})+O(N^{4\kappa-1})\,.\end{split}

∎
